================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================
# Irodori Bunpou — Pembelajaran Tata Bahasa Jepang (A1–A2)

Aplikasi web interaktif berbahasa Indonesia untuk mempelajari tata bahasa Jepang (**Bunpou**) dari 3 buku resmi **Irodori: Bahasa Jepang untuk Kehidupan di Jepang** (*Japan Foundation*):

- **Pemula (入門 / A1)**: Bab 3–18 (16 Bab · 82 Pola)
- **Dasar 1 (初級1 / A2)**: Bab 1–18 (18 Bab · 95 Pola)
- **Dasar 2 (初級2 / A2)**: Bab 1–18 (18 Bab · 87 Pola)
- **Total**: **52 Bab · 264 Pola Tata Bahasa · 792 Contoh Kalimat** lengkap dengan markup Furigana `{漢字|かな}` dan padanan 『まるごと』.

## Fitur Utama

- **Kartu Hafalan 3D (Flashcard)**: Animasi flip 3D, pintasan keyboard (`Spasi`, `←`/`→`, `H` Hafal, `U` Sulit), filter status (*Semua / Belum Hafal / Tanda Sulit*), dan acak dek.
- **Daftar Bunpou & Pencarian Instan**: Pencarian cepat terindeks berdasarkan pola, rumus, fungsi, hiragana/kanji, maupun terjemahan Indonesia, lengkap dengan kode warna rumus dan audio pelafalan Jepang (Web Speech API `ja-JP`).
- **Kuis Latihan (JFT-Basic / JLPT)**: Pilihan ganda Rumpang (*Cloze* dengan Furigana terjaga), Rumus Penyambungan, Fungsi Pola, dan Campuran — dilengkapi statistik skor dan riwayat 15 sesi terakhir.
- **Sinkronisasi Cloud & Mode Offline**: Login Google / GitHub / Email via Firebase Auth untuk sinkronisasi progres & riwayat kuis lintas perangkat, serta **Backup / Restore JSON** lokal yang tetap tersedia baik saat login maupun tamu.
- **Tema Terang & Gelap** serta toggle **Furigana** global.

## Menjalankan Secara Lokal

```bash
npm install --legacy-peer-deps
npm run dev
```

## Pengujian & Validasi

```bash
npm test            # Unit test logika bunpou, kuis, storage, dataset, & rules
npm run lint        # Pengecekan TypeScript (tsc --noEmit)
npm run lint:rules  # Validasi keamanan firestore.rules
npm run build       # Build produksi Vite
```

================================================
FILE: firestore.rules
================================================
rules_version = '2';

service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    // Global Safety Net: Default-deny catch-all
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    // --- Reusable Security Primitives ---
    function incoming() {
      return request.resource.data;
    }

    function existing() {
      return resource.data;
    }

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isVerifiedUser() {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.token.email_verified == true;
    }

    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() >= 1 && id.size() <= 128 && id.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$');
    }

    function isOwner(userId) {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;
    }

    function isValidBookId(bookId) {
      return bookId is string && bookId in ['introductory', 'elementary1', 'elementary2'];
    }

    function isValidDisplayName(data) {
      return !('displayName' in data)
        || (data.displayName is string && data.displayName.size() <= 60);
    }

    function isValidAvatarUrl(data) {
      return !('avatarUrl' in data)
        || (data.avatarUrl is string
          && data.avatarUrl.size() <= 20000
          && (data.avatarUrl.size() == 0
            || data.avatarUrl.matches('^https://[^\\s]+$')
            || data.avatarUrl.matches('^data:image/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$')));
    }

    // Full-array validation without loops using dual-separator join (',' and '|'):
    // Enforces list type, max size (<= 150), uniqueness (toSet), and exact per-book Bunpou pattern ID format.
    function isValidIntroductoryList(arr) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 150
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (
          arr.size() == 0
          || (
            arr[0] is string
            && arr[0].matches('^introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$')
            && arr.join(',').size() <= 4000
            && arr.join(',').matches('^introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?(,introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?)*$')
            && arr.join('|').matches('^introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?(\\|introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?)*$')
          )
        );
    }

    function isValidElementary1List(arr) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 150
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (
          arr.size() == 0
          || (
            arr[0] is string
            && arr[0].matches('^elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$')
            && arr.join(',').size() <= 4000
            && arr.join(',').matches('^elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?(,elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?)*$')
            && arr.join('|').matches('^elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?(\\|elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?)*$')
          )
        );
    }

    function isValidElementary2List(arr) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 150
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (
          arr.size() == 0
          || (
            arr[0] is string
            && arr[0].matches('^elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$')
            && arr.join(',').size() <= 4000
            && arr.join(',').matches('^elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?(,elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?)*$')
            && arr.join('|').matches('^elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?(\\|elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?)*$')
          )
        );
    }

    function isValidBunpouList(arr, bookId) {
      return (bookId == 'introductory' && isValidIntroductoryList(arr))
        || (bookId == 'elementary1' && isValidElementary1List(arr))
        || (bookId == 'elementary2' && isValidElementary2List(arr));
    }

    // Compact pure-ASCII quiz session token validation (per-book patternId prefix enforced):
    // Format: id|completedAt|focus|correct|total|score|scopeCode|answersPacked
    // answersPacked items separated by ';': no:typeCode:patternId:isCorrectBit
    // typeCode: 0=cloze, 1=formula, 2=function; isCorrectBit: 1=right, 0=wrong
    function isValidIntroductoryQuizToken(val) {
      return val is string
        && val.size() >= 24
        && val.size() <= 8000
        && val.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(mixed|cloze|formula|function)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(all|([1-9]|1[0-8])(\\.([1-9]|1[0-8]))*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-2]:introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?:[01](;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-2]:introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?:[01])*)$');
    }

    function isValidElementary1QuizToken(val) {
      return val is string
        && val.size() >= 24
        && val.size() <= 8000
        && val.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(mixed|cloze|formula|function)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(all|([1-9]|1[0-8])(\\.([1-9]|1[0-8]))*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-2]:elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?:[01](;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-2]:elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?:[01])*)$');
    }

    function isValidElementary2QuizToken(val) {
      return val is string
        && val.size() >= 24
        && val.size() <= 8000
        && val.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(mixed|cloze|formula|function)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(all|([1-9]|1[0-8])(\\.([1-9]|1[0-8]))*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-2]:elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?:[01](;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-2]:elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?:[01])*)$');
    }

    function isValidQuizSessionToken(val, bookId) {
      return (bookId == 'introductory' && isValidIntroductoryQuizToken(val))
        || (bookId == 'elementary1' && isValidElementary1QuizToken(val))
        || (bookId == 'elementary2' && isValidElementary2QuizToken(val));
    }

    // Element-wise bounded validation across all 15 slots avoids running giant regexes over a 120 KB joined string
    function isValidQuizHistorySessions(arr, bookId) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 15
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (arr.size() <= 0 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[0], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 1 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[1], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 2 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[2], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 3 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[3], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 4 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[4], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 5 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[5], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 6 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[6], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 7 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[7], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 8 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[8], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 9 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[9], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 10 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[10], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 11 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[11], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 12 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[12], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 13 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[13], bookId))
        && (arr.size() <= 14 || isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[14], bookId));
    }

    // Standalone Validation Blueprint for UserProfile (safe for both create and update)
    function isValidUserProfile(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'displayName', 'avatarUrl', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.uid is string
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && data.uid == request.auth.uid
        && isValidDisplayName(data)
        && isValidAvatarUrl(data)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Standalone Validation Blueprint for BunpouBookProgress (safe for both create and update)
    function isValidBunpouProgress(data, bookId) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'bookId', 'mastered', 'hard', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'bookId', 'mastered', 'hard', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.uid is string
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && data.uid == request.auth.uid
        && isValidBookId(data.bookId)
        && data.bookId == bookId
        && isValidBunpouList(data.mastered, bookId)
        && isValidBunpouList(data.hard, bookId)
        && data.mastered.toSet().intersection(data.hard.toSet()).size() == 0
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Standalone Validation Blueprint for BunpouQuizHistory (safe for both create and update)
    function isValidBunpouQuizHistory(data, bookId) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'bookId', 'sessions', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'bookId', 'sessions', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.uid is string
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && data.uid == request.auth.uid
        && isValidBookId(data.bookId)
        && data.bookId == bookId
        && isValidQuizHistorySessions(data.sessions, bookId)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // --- /users/{userId} ---
    match /users/{userId} {
      allow get: if isSignedIn()
        && isValidId(userId)
        && isOwner(userId);

      allow list: if false;

      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && isVerifiedUser()
        && isValidId(userId)
        && isOwner(userId)
        && isValidUserProfile(incoming())
        && incoming().uid == userId
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;

      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && isVerifiedUser()
        && isValidId(userId)
        && isOwner(userId)
        && isValidUserProfile(incoming())
        && incoming().uid == existing().uid
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['displayName', 'avatarUrl', 'updatedAt']);

      // Deny client-side profile deletion to prevent orphaned subcollections
      allow delete: if false;

      // --- /users/{userId}/bunpouProgress/{bookId} ---
      match /bunpouProgress/{bookId} {
        allow get: if isSignedIn()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidBookId(bookId)
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow list: if false;

        allow create: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidBookId(bookId)
          && isValidBunpouProgress(incoming(), bookId)
          && incoming().uid == userId
          && incoming().createdAt == request.time
          && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow update: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidBookId(bookId)
          && isValidBunpouProgress(incoming(), bookId)
          && incoming().uid == existing().uid
          && incoming().bookId == existing().bookId
          && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
          && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
          && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['mastered', 'hard', 'updatedAt'])
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow delete: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidBookId(bookId)
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));
      }

      // --- /users/{userId}/quizHistory/{bookId} ---
      match /quizHistory/{bookId} {
        allow get: if isSignedIn()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidBookId(bookId)
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow list: if false;

        allow create: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidBookId(bookId)
          && isValidBunpouQuizHistory(incoming(), bookId)
          && incoming().uid == userId
          && incoming().createdAt == request.time
          && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow update: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidBookId(bookId)
          && isValidBunpouQuizHistory(incoming(), bookId)
          && incoming().uid == existing().uid
          && incoming().bookId == existing().bookId
          && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
          && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
          && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['sessions', 'updatedAt'])
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow delete: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidBookId(bookId)
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));
      }
    }
  }
}



================================================
FILE: firestore.rules.test.ts
================================================
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const RULES_PATH = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'firestore.rules');
const rulesText = fs.readFileSync(RULES_PATH, 'utf8');

const INTRO_ITEM_RE = /^introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$/;
const ELEM1_ITEM_RE = /^elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$/;
const ELEM2_ITEM_RE = /^elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$/;

function buildQuizTokenRegex(bookId: string): RegExp {
  return new RegExp(
    `^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(mixed|cloze|formula|function)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(all|([1-9]|1[0-8])(\\.([1-9]|1[0-8]))*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-2]:${bookId}-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?:[01](;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-2]:${bookId}-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?:[01])*)$`
  );
}

interface MockAuth {
  uid: string;
  emailVerified: boolean;
}

function validateList(arr: unknown, bookId: string): boolean {
  if (!Array.isArray(arr) || arr.length > 150) return false;
  if (new Set(arr).size !== arr.length) return false;
  if (arr.length === 0) return true;
  if (!arr.every(v => typeof v === 'string')) return false;
  const re =
    bookId === 'introductory'
      ? INTRO_ITEM_RE
      : bookId === 'elementary1'
        ? ELEM1_ITEM_RE
        : bookId === 'elementary2'
          ? ELEM2_ITEM_RE
          : null;
  if (!re) return false;
  const joinedComma = arr.join(',');
  const joinedPipe = arr.join('|');
  if (joinedComma.length > 4000) return false;
  const singleSrc = re.source.slice(1, -1);
  const commaRe = new RegExp(`^${singleSrc}(,${singleSrc})*$`);
  const pipeRe = new RegExp(`^${singleSrc}(\\|${singleSrc})*$`);
  return re.test(arr[0]) && commaRe.test(joinedComma) && pipeRe.test(joinedPipe);
}

function validateSessions(arr: unknown, bookId: string): boolean {
  if (!['introductory', 'elementary1', 'elementary2'].includes(bookId)) return false;
  if (!Array.isArray(arr) || arr.length > 15) return false;
  if (new Set(arr).size !== arr.length) return false;
  const tokenRe = buildQuizTokenRegex(bookId);
  return arr.every(
    item =>
      typeof item === 'string' &&
      item.length >= 24 &&
      item.length <= 8000 &&
      tokenRe.test(item)
  );
}

function canWriteProgress(
  auth: MockAuth | null,
  userId: string,
  bookId: string,
  parentExists: boolean,
  data: Record<string, unknown>,
  nowMarker = 'SERVER_TIME'
): boolean {
  if (!auth || !auth.emailVerified) return false;
  if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]{1,128}$/.test(userId)) return false;
  if (auth.uid !== userId) return false;
  if (!['introductory', 'elementary1', 'elementary2'].includes(bookId)) return false;
  const keys = Object.keys(data);
  const required = ['uid', 'bookId', 'mastered', 'hard', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'];
  if (!required.every(k => keys.includes(k))) return false;
  if (!keys.every(k => required.includes(k))) return false;
  if (data.uid !== auth.uid || data.bookId !== bookId) return false;
  if (data.createdAt !== nowMarker || data.updatedAt !== nowMarker) return false;
  if (!validateList(data.mastered, bookId) || !validateList(data.hard, bookId)) return false;
  const mSet = new Set(data.mastered as string[]);
  for (const h of data.hard as string[]) {
    if (mSet.has(h)) return false;
  }
  return parentExists;
}

test('firestore.rules structural security invariants (Eight Pillars + Hardening)', () => {
  assert.match(rulesText, /rules_version = '2';/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /match \/\{document=\*\*\} \{\s*allow read, write: if false;\s*\}/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /request\.auth\.token\.email_verified == true/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /allow list: if false;/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /allow delete: if false;/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /exists\(\/databases\/\$\(database\)\/documents\/users\/\$\(userId\)\)/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /incoming\(\)\.createdAt == request\.time/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /incoming\(\)\.updatedAt == request\.time/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /incoming\(\)\.diff\(existing\(\)\)\.affectedKeys\(\)\.hasOnly/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /isValidIntroductoryQuizToken/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /isValidElementary1QuizToken/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /isValidElementary2QuizToken/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /isValidQuizSessionToken\(arr\[14\], bookId\)/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /match \/bunpouProgress\/\{bookId\}/);
  assert.match(rulesText, /match \/quizHistory\/\{bookId\}/);
});

test('Dirty Dozen adversarial payloads return PERMISSION_DENIED', () => {
  const validAuth: MockAuth = { uid: 'userA', emailVerified: true };
  const baseValid: Record<string, unknown> = {
    uid: 'userA',
    bookId: 'introductory',
    mastered: ['introductory-bab-3-p1', 'introductory-bab-4-p3'],
    hard: ['introductory-bab-5-p1'],
    createdAt: 'SERVER_TIME',
    updatedAt: 'SERVER_TIME',
  };

  assert.equal(canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, baseValid), true);

  // 1. Unauthenticated access
  assert.equal(canWriteProgress(null, 'userA', 'introductory', true, baseValid), false);

  // 2. Unverified email write
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress({ uid: 'userA', emailVerified: false }, 'userA', 'introductory', true, baseValid),
    false
  );

  // 3. Cross-user access
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress({ uid: 'userB', emailVerified: true }, 'userA', 'introductory', true, baseValid),
    false
  );

  // 4. Identity & BookId spoofing
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'advanced1', true, { ...baseValid, bookId: 'advanced1' }),
    false
  );
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, { ...baseValid, bookId: 'elementary1' }),
    false
  );

  // 5. Master Gate bypass (parent /users/userA missing)
  assert.equal(canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', false, baseValid), false);

  // 6. Shadow / Ghost field injection
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, { ...baseValid, isAdmin: true }),
    false
  );

  // 7. Forged client timestamp
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, {
      ...baseValid,
      updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',
    }),
    false
  );

  // 8. Full-array XSS & separator smuggling at index > 0
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, {
      ...baseValid,
      mastered: ['introductory-bab-3-p1', '<script>alert(1)</script>'],
    }),
    false
  );
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, {
      ...baseValid,
      mastered: ['introductory-bab-3-p1', 'introductory-bab-3-p2,introductory-bab-3-p3'],
    }),
    false
  );
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, {
      ...baseValid,
      mastered: ['introductory-bab-3-p1', 'introductory-bab-3-p2|introductory-bab-3-p3'],
    }),
    false
  );

  // 9. Cross-book prefix spoofing & out-of-range chapter
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, {
      ...baseValid,
      mastered: ['elementary1-bab-1-p1'],
    }),
    false
  );
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, {
      ...baseValid,
      mastered: ['introductory-bab-19-p1'],
    }),
    false
  );

  // 10. Duplicates & mastered/hard overlap
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, {
      ...baseValid,
      mastered: ['introductory-bab-3-p1', 'introductory-bab-3-p1'],
    }),
    false
  );
  assert.equal(
    canWriteProgress(validAuth, 'userA', 'introductory', true, {
      ...baseValid,
      mastered: ['introductory-bab-3-p1'],
      hard: ['introductory-bab-3-p1'],
    }),
    false
  );

  // 11. Quiz history token validation, per-book prefix enforcement, & separator smuggling
  const validIntroToken =
    'q1700000000_ab12|2026-11-19T04:00:00.000Z|mixed|8|10|80|3.4.5|1:0:introductory-bab-3-p1:1;2:1:introductory-bab-4-p1:0';
  assert.equal(validateSessions([validIntroToken], 'introductory'), true);
  // Cross-book prefix inside quizHistory must fail
  assert.equal(validateSessions([validIntroToken], 'elementary1'), false);
  assert.equal(
    validateSessions([validIntroToken, `${validIntroToken},${validIntroToken}`], 'introductory'),
    false
  );
  assert.equal(
    validateSessions([validIntroToken, `${validIntroToken}~${validIntroToken}`], 'introductory'),
    false
  );
  assert.equal(
    validateSessions(
      ['q1|2026-11-19T04:00:00.000Z|mixed|8|10|105|all|1:0:introductory-bab-3-p1:1'],
      'introductory'
    ),
    false
  );
  assert.equal(
    validateSessions(
      Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, i) => `q${i}|2026-11-19T04:00:00.000Z|mixed|1|1|100|all|`),
      'introductory'
    ),
    false
  );

  // 12. Unsafe avatar URLs
  const avatarHttpsRe = /^https:\/\/[^\s]+$/;
  const avatarDataRe = /^data:image\/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$/;
  assert.equal(avatarHttpsRe.test('javascript:alert(1)'), false);
  assert.equal(avatarDataRe.test('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2Zz4='), false);
  assert.equal(avatarDataRe.test('data:image/jpeg;base64,/9j/4AAQSkZJRg=='), true);
});

FILE: security_spec.md
================================================
# Security Specification — Irodori Bunpou Cloud Sync

## 1. Data Invariants

### A. `/users/{userId}` (`UserProfile`)
1. **Strict Ownership**: Every document in `/users/{userId}` belongs strictly to the authenticated user where `request.auth.uid == userId` and `data.uid == request.auth.uid`. Cross-user `get`, `list`, `create`, `update`, and `delete` are denied.
2. **Verified Identity**: All mutations (`create`, `update`) require `request.auth != null && request.auth.token.email_verified == true`.
3. **Closed Schema & Action-Based Updates**: Only `uid`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt`, and optional `displayName` (`<= 60` chars) / `avatarUrl` (`<= 20,000` chars, strictly `""`, `https://...`, or `data:image/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,...`) are permitted. Updates may only affect `['displayName', 'avatarUrl', 'updatedAt']`. Shadow fields (`role`, `isAdmin`, `email`, etc.) are rejected.
4. **Immutable Identity & Server Timestamps**: `uid` and `createdAt` can never change after creation; `createdAt` (on create) and `updatedAt` (on create/update) must equal `request.time`.
5. **Zero Listing & No Client Delete**: `allow list: if false;` prevents user enumeration, and `allow delete: if false;` prevents leaving orphaned `bunpouProgress` / `quizHistory` subcollections.

### B. `/users/{userId}/bunpouProgress/{bookId}` (`BunpouBookProgress`)
1. **Master Gate**: All operations (`get`, `create`, `update`, `delete`) require the parent `/users/{userId}` document to exist (`exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId))`), evaluated after all cheap static checks.
2. **Strict Book Allowlist**: `bookId` path variable and `data.bookId` field must match and be strictly in `['introductory', 'elementary1', 'elementary2']`.
3. **Closed Schema & Immutability**: Only `uid`, `bookId`, `mastered`, `hard`, `createdAt`, and `updatedAt` are permitted. Updates may only mutate `['mastered', 'hard', 'updatedAt']`; `uid`, `bookId`, and `createdAt` are immutable.
4. **Per-Book Full-Array Validation**: Every element of `mastered` and `hard` is validated via uniqueness (`toSet()`), mutual exclusivity (`intersection().size() == 0`), size limit (`<= 150` items — covering all 82/95/87 patterns per book), and dual-separator join (`','` and `'|'`) against exact per-book pattern ID regexes:
   - `introductory`: `^introductory-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$` (`join(',').size() <= 4000`)
   - `elementary1`: `^elementary1-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$` (`join(',').size() <= 4000`)
   - `elementary2`: `^elementary2-bab-([1-9]|1[0-8])-p[1-9][0-9]?$` (`join(',').size() <= 4000`)
5. **Zero Listing**: `allow list: if false;` — client reads only the 3 known book IDs directly.

### C. `/users/{userId}/quizHistory/{bookId}` (`BunpouQuizHistory`)
1. **Master Gate**: All operations (`get`, `create`, `update`, `delete`) require `/users/{userId}` to exist (`exists(...)`), evaluated after all cheap static checks.
2. **Strict Book Allowlist**: `bookId` path variable and `data.bookId` must match and be in `['introductory', 'elementary1', 'elementary2']`.
3. **Closed Schema & Immutability**: Only `uid`, `bookId`, `sessions`, `createdAt`, and `updatedAt` are permitted. Updates may only mutate `['sessions', 'updatedAt']`.
4. **Compact Pure-ASCII Element-Wise Token Validation**: `sessions` must be a unique list of `<= 15` compact ASCII tokens (`24..8000` chars each), validated individually across all 15 bounded slots (`arr[0]..arr[14]`) with per-book `patternId` prefix matching against strict token grammar:
   `id|completedAt|focus|correct|total|score|scopeCode|answersPacked`
   Zero free-form user text, HTML, or JSON strings are stored; full pattern titles, formulas, and explanations are hydrated client-side from the static Irodori Bunpou dataset.
5. **Zero Listing**: `allow list: if false;`.

## 2. The "Dirty Dozen" Adversarial Payloads (`firestore.rules.test.ts`)
1. **Unauthenticated Access**: `auth = null` attempting read/write on `/users/userA` or subcollections -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
2. **Unverified Email Write**: `email_verified = false` attempting create/update -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
3. **Cross-User Access**: `userB` attempting get/create/update/delete on `userA` documents -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
4. **Collection Scraping (`list`) & Profile Orphan Delete**: Listing `/users`, `/users/{uid}/bunpouProgress`, or `/users/{uid}/quizHistory`, or deleting `/users/{uid}` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
5. **Identity & BookId Spoofing**: Mismatched `uid`, mismatched `bookId`, or unknown `bookId` (`advanced1`) -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
6. **Master Gate Bypass**: Accessing `bunpouProgress` or `quizHistory` when `/users/{uid}` does not exist -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
7. **Shadow / Ghost Field Injection**: Adding `role: 'admin'` or `isAdmin: true` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
8. **Immortal Field Tampering & Forged Timestamps**: Modifying `uid`, `bookId`, `createdAt`, or sending client timestamp instead of `request.time` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
9. **Full-Array XSS & Separator Smuggling (Index > 0)**: Injecting `['introductory-bab-1-p1', '<script>alert(1)</script>']` or `['introductory-bab-1-p1', 'introductory-bab-1-p2,introductory-bab-1-p3']` or `~` / `,` smuggling inside `sessions` at index > 0 -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
10. **Cross-Book Prefix Spoofing & Out-of-Range Values**: Writing `elementary1-bab-1-p1` inside `introductory` (both `bunpouProgress` and `quizHistory`), `bab-19`, `score > 100`, or `> 15` sessions -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
11. **Duplicates & Mastered/Hard Overlap**: Duplicate IDs or identical IDs in both `mastered` and `hard` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
12. **Unsafe Profile Inputs**: `javascript:alert(1)`, `data:image/svg+xml`, or `displayName > 60` chars -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.

FILE: vite.config.ts

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    base: process.env.VITE_BASE_PATH || '/',
    plugins: [
      react(),
      tailwindcss(),
      // Copies src/assets into dist/ so root-relative URLs like
      // "/src/assets/images/..." (e.g. generate_image output) still resolve
      // in production builds. Do not remove.
      {
        name: 'copy-unbundled-src-assets',
        apply: 'build',
        closeBundle(error?: Error): void {
          if (error) {
            return;
          }
          const distDir = path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist');
          const srcAssets = path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/assets');
          const distAssets = path.resolve(distDir, 'src/assets');
          if (fs.existsSync(distDir) && fs.existsSync(srcAssets)) {
            fs.cpSync(srcAssets, distAssets, {recursive: true, force: true});
          }
        },
      },
    ],
    build: {
      rollupOptions: {
        output: {
          manualChunks(id) {
            const normalized = id.replace(/\\/g, '/');
            if (
              normalized.includes('node_modules/firebase') ||
              normalized.includes('node_modules/@firebase')
            ) {
              return 'firebase-vendor';
            }
            if (normalized.includes('/src/data/irodori')) {
              return 'irodori-data';
            }
            return undefined;
          },
        },
      },
    },
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

================================================
FILE: src/App.tsx
================================================
import { Suspense, lazy, useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { AppHeader } from './components/AppHeader';
import { AuthModal } from './components/AuthModal';
import { BunpouListPanel } from './components/BunpouListPanel';
import { FlashcardPanel } from './components/FlashcardPanel';
import { IrodoriBookMenu } from './components/IrodoriBookMenu';
import { Modal } from './components/Modal';
import { ProfileModal } from './components/ProfileModal';
import { RestoreModal } from './components/RestoreModal';
import { Sidebar } from './components/Sidebar';
import { ToastBanner } from './components/ToastBanner';
import { IRODORI_DATASET } from './data/irodoriDataset';
import { getFirestoreUpgradeUrl } from './firebase';
import { useAuthActions } from './hooks/useAuthActions';
import { useBackupRestore } from './hooks/useBackupRestore';
import { useCloudProgressSync } from './hooks/useCloudProgressSync';
import { useFlashcardDeck } from './hooks/useFlashcardDeck';
import { useTheme } from './hooks/useTheme';
import { useToast } from './hooks/useToast';
import type { AppTab, BookId, BunpouPattern, ChapterFilter } from './types/bunpou';
import { createBookRecord } from './utils/bunpouSync';
import { stopSpeaking } from './utils/speech';
import {
  loadGlobalFuriganaPref,
  loadGlobalTranslationPref,
  saveGlobalFuriganaPref,
  saveGlobalTranslationPref,
} from './utils/storage';

const QuizPanel = lazy(() =>
  import('./components/QuizPanel').then(m => ({ default: m.QuizPanel }))
);
const AbbreviationsView = lazy(() =>
  import('./components/AbbreviationsView').then(m => ({ default: m.AbbreviationsView }))
);

function LazyTabFallback() {
  return (
    <div
      role="status"
      aria-label="Memuat tampilan"
      className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shadow-sm"
    >
      Memuat modul belajar...
    </div>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  const dataset = IRODORI_DATASET;

  const [activeBookId, setActiveBookId] = useState<BookId>('introductory');
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<AppTab>('card');
  const [activeChapter, setActiveChapter] = useState<ChapterFilter>('all');
  const [showFurigana, setShowFurigana] = useState<boolean>(() => loadGlobalFuriganaPref());
  const [showTranslation, setShowTranslation] = useState<boolean>(() => loadGlobalTranslationPref());
  const [resetConfirmOpen, setResetConfirmOpen] = useState(false);

  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const { toast, showToast, dismissToast } = useToast();

  const toggleFurigana = useCallback(() => {
    setShowFurigana(prev => {
      const next = !prev;
      saveGlobalFuriganaPref(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const toggleTranslation = useCallback(() => {
    setShowTranslation(prev => {
      const next = !prev;
      saveGlobalTranslationPref(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const validIdsByBook = useMemo<Record<BookId, Set<string>>>(() => {
    const map = createBookRecord(() => new Set<string>());
    for (const p of dataset.allPatterns) {
      map[p.bookId].add(p.id);
    }
    return map;
  }, [dataset]);

  const patternById = useMemo<Map<string, BunpouPattern>>(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, BunpouPattern>();
    for (const p of dataset.allPatterns) {
      map.set(p.id, p);
    }
    return map;
  }, [dataset]);

  const authActions = useAuthActions({ showToast });

  const cloudSync = useCloudProgressSync({
    user: authActions.user,
    authReady: authActions.authReady,
    authVersion: authActions.authVersion,
    justSignedInRef: authActions.justSignedInRef,
    activeTab,
    activeBookId,
    validIdsByBook,
    patternById,
    datasetReady: true,
    showToast,
  });

  const activeBook = useMemo(
    () => dataset.books.find(b => b.id === activeBookId) ?? dataset.books[0],
    [dataset, activeBookId]
  );

  const activeBucket = cloudSync.booksProgress[activeBookId];
  const activeMastered = activeBucket.mastered;
  const activeHardSet = activeBucket.hard;
  const activeValidIds = validIdsByBook[activeBookId];

  const handleToggleMastered = useCallback(
    (patternId: string) => {
      const nextM = new Set(activeMastered);
      const nextH = new Set(activeHardSet);
      if (nextM.has(patternId)) {
        nextM.delete(patternId);
      } else {
        nextM.add(patternId);
        nextH.delete(patternId);
      }
      cloudSync.applyProgressChange(nextM, nextH, activeBookId);
    },
    [activeMastered, activeHardSet, activeBookId, cloudSync]
  );

  const handleToggleHard = useCallback(
    (patternId: string) => {
      const nextM = new Set(activeMastered);
      const nextH = new Set(activeHardSet);
      if (nextH.has(patternId)) {
        nextH.delete(patternId);
      } else {
        nextH.add(patternId);
        nextM.delete(patternId);
      }
      cloudSync.applyProgressChange(nextM, nextH, activeBookId);
    },
    [activeMastered, activeHardSet, activeBookId, cloudSync]
  );

  const resetDeckToFirstRef = useRef<() => void>(() => undefined);

  const backupRestore = useBackupRestore({
    bookId: activeBookId,
    activeMastered,
    activeHardSet,
    activeValidIds,
    applyProgressChange: (m, h) => cloudSync.applyProgressChange(m, h, activeBookId),
    onRestoreCommitted: () => resetDeckToFirstRef.current(),
    showToast,
    dismissToast,
  });

  const deck = useFlashcardDeck({
    activeBookId,
    activeBook,
    activeTab,
    activeChapter,
    activeMastered,
    activeHardSet,
    shortcutsDisabled:
      authActions.authModalOpen ||
      cloudSync.profileModalOpen ||
      Boolean(backupRestore.pendingRestore) ||
      resetConfirmOpen,
    onToggleMastered: handleToggleMastered,
    onToggleHard: handleToggleHard,
    showToast,
  });

  resetDeckToFirstRef.current = deck.resetDeckToFirst;

  const handleSelectBook = useCallback(
    (nextBookId: BookId) => {
      stopSpeaking();
      setActiveBookId(nextBookId);
      setActiveChapter('all');
      deck.resetDeckForBook();
    },
    [deck.resetDeckForBook]
  );

  const handleSelectChapter = useCallback(
    (nextChapter: ChapterFilter) => {
      stopSpeaking();
      setActiveChapter(nextChapter);
      deck.resetDeckForChapter();
    },
    [deck.resetDeckForChapter]
  );

  const handleSelectTab = useCallback((nextTab: AppTab) => {
    stopSpeaking();
    setActiveTab(nextTab);
  }, []);

  const handleConfirmResetBook = useCallback(() => {
    cloudSync.applyProgressChange(new Set(), new Set(), activeBookId);
    deck.resetDeckAfterBookReset();
    setResetConfirmOpen(false);
    showToast(
      'info',
      'Progres Buku Direset',
      `Status hafal & sulit untuk ${activeBook.shortTitle} telah dikosongkan.`
    );
  }, [activeBook.shortTitle, activeBookId, cloudSync, deck.resetDeckAfterBookReset, showToast]);

  const handleSignOut = useCallback(() => {
    void authActions.executeSignOut(() => {
      cloudSync.resetCloudAndLocalProgress();
      backupRestore.clearPendingRestore();
      deck.resetDeckToFirst();
    });
  }, [authActions, cloudSync, backupRestore, deck.resetDeckToFirst]);

  const chapterLabel = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeChapter === 'all') {
      const firstCh = activeBook.chapters[0]?.chapterNumber ?? 1;
      const lastCh = activeBook.chapters[activeBook.chapters.length - 1]?.chapterNumber ?? 18;
      return `Semua Bab (${firstCh}–${lastCh})`;
    }
    const ch = activeBook.chapters.find(c => c.chapterNumber === activeChapter);
    return ch ? `Bab ${ch.chapterNumber}: ${ch.titleId}` : `Bab ${activeChapter}`;
  }, [activeBook, activeChapter]);

  const totalChaptersCount = useMemo(
    () => dataset.books.reduce((sum, b) => sum + b.chapters.length, 0),
    [dataset]
  );

  const totalExamplesCount = useMemo(
    () => dataset.allPatterns.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.examples.length, 0),
    [dataset]
  );

  const resolvedDisplayName =
    cloudSync.customDisplayName ||
    authActions.user?.displayName ||
    authActions.user?.email?.split('@')[0] ||
    '';
  const resolvedAvatarUrl = cloudSync.customAvatarUrl || authActions.user?.photoURL || '';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 flex flex-col transition-colors">
      <AppHeader
        bookShortTitle={activeBook.shortTitle}
        masteredCount={activeMastered.size}
        totalCount={activeBook.totalPatterns}
        preferences={{
          showFurigana,
          onToggleFurigana: toggleFurigana,
          showTranslation,
          onToggleTranslation: toggleTranslation,
          isDark,
          onToggleTheme: toggleTheme,
        }}
        account={{
          user: authActions.user,
          displayName: resolvedDisplayName,
          avatarUrl: resolvedAvatarUrl,
          authReady: authActions.authReady,
          authBusy: authActions.authBusy,
          syncing: cloudSync.syncing,
          onOpenAuthModal: authActions.handleOpenAuthModal,
          onOpenProfileModal: () => cloudSync.setProfileModalOpen(true),
          onManualSync: () => void cloudSync.handleManualSync(),
          onResendVerification: () => void authActions.handleResendVerification(),
          onRefreshVerification: () => void authActions.handleRefreshVerification(),
          onSignOut: handleSignOut,
        }}
        backup={{
          restoreBtnRef: backupRestore.restoreBtnRef,
          fileInputRef: backupRestore.fileInputRef,
          onExport: backupRestore.handleExport,
          onTriggerImport: backupRestore.handleTriggerImport,
          onFileSelected: backupRestore.handleFileSelected,
        }}
      />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-6xl w-full mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-4 sm:py-6 space-y-4 sm:space-y-5">
        {cloudSync.fatalError ? (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/60 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/80 p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3"
          >
            <div className="space-y-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-amber-950 dark:text-amber-100">
              <p className="font-bold">Batas Kuota Harian Firestore Tercapai</p>
              <p className="text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200 leading-relaxed">
                Sinkronisasi cloud dijeda sementara hingga kuota direset. Seluruh materi dan
                progres tetap aman di penyimpanan lokal perangkat ini.{' '}
                <a
                  href={getFirestoreUpgradeUrl()}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="underline font-semibold"
                >
                  Kelola kuota Firebase
                </a>
                .
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={cloudSync.enableOfflineFallback}
              className="min-h-[38px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
            >
              Lanjut Mode Offline
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <IrodoriBookMenu
          books={dataset.books}
          activeBookId={activeBookId}
          booksProgress={cloudSync.booksProgress}
          onSelectBook={handleSelectBook}
        />

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[260px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-start">
          <Sidebar
            activeTab={activeTab}
            activeChapter={activeChapter}
            book={activeBook}
            masteredSet={activeMastered}
            hardSet={activeHardSet}
            onSelectTab={handleSelectTab}
            onSelectChapter={handleSelectChapter}
          />

          <div className="min-w-0">
            {activeTab === 'card' ? (
              <FlashcardPanel
                deck={deck.flashcardDeck}
                currentIndex={deck.cardIndex}
                isFlipped={deck.isFlipped}
                showFurigana={showFurigana}
                showTranslation={showTranslation}
                deckFilter={deck.deckFilter}
                masteredSet={activeMastered}
                hardSet={activeHardSet}
                bookShortTitle={activeBook.shortTitle}
                chapterLabel={chapterLabel}
                onChangeDeckFilter={deck.handleChangeDeckFilter}
                onFlip={deck.handleFlipCard}
                onPrev={deck.handlePrevCard}
                onNext={deck.handleNextCard}
                onShuffle={deck.handleShuffleDeck}
                onMarkMastered={handleToggleMastered}
                onMarkHard={handleToggleHard}
                onResetBookProgress={() => setResetConfirmOpen(true)}
              />
            ) : activeTab === 'list' ? (
              <BunpouListPanel
                patterns={deck.scopedPatterns}
                showFurigana={showFurigana}
                showTranslation={showTranslation}
                masteredSet={activeMastered}
                hardSet={activeHardSet}
                bookShortTitle={activeBook.shortTitle}
                chapterLabel={chapterLabel}
                onToggleMastered={handleToggleMastered}
                onToggleHard={handleToggleHard}
              />
            ) : activeTab === 'quiz' ? (
              <Suspense fallback={<LazyTabFallback />}>
                <QuizPanel
                  book={activeBook}
                  allPatterns={dataset.allPatterns}
                  showFurigana={showFurigana}
                  showTranslation={showTranslation}
                  history={cloudSync.quizHistories[activeBookId] ?? []}
                  historySyncing={cloudSync.quizHistorySyncing}
                  cloudEnabled={
                    Boolean(authActions.user?.emailVerified) && !cloudSync.offlineFallback
                  }
                  onRecordQuizSession={cloudSync.handleRecordQuizSession}
                  onClearQuizHistory={() => cloudSync.handleClearQuizHistory(activeBookId)}
                />
              </Suspense>
            ) : (
              <Suspense fallback={<LazyTabFallback />}>
                <AbbreviationsView
                  abbreviations={dataset.abbreviations}
                  books={dataset.books}
                  booksProgress={cloudSync.booksProgress}
                />
              </Suspense>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      <footer className="border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white/70 dark:bg-slate-900/60 py-4 px-4 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
        Irodori Bunpou (Pemula · Dasar 1 · Dasar 2) · {totalChaptersCount} Bab ·{' '}
        {dataset.allPatterns.length} Pola Tata Bahasa · {totalExamplesCount} Contoh Kalimat
      </footer>

      {/* Reset Book Progress Confirmation Modal */}
      <Modal
        open={resetConfirmOpen}
        onClose={() => setResetConfirmOpen(false)}
        id="resetConfirmModal"
        ariaLabelledBy="resetConfirmTitle"
        panelClassName="p-6 space-y-4"
      >
        <h2
          id="resetConfirmTitle"
          className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50"
        >
          Reset Progres {activeBook.shortTitle}?
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
          Semua tanda <strong>Sudah Hafal ({activeMastered.size})</strong> dan{' '}
          <strong>Sulit ({activeHardSet.size})</strong> untuk buku{' '}
          <strong>{activeBook.shortTitle}</strong> akan dikosongkan. Progres buku lainnya tidak
          terpengaruh.
        </p>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setResetConfirmOpen(false)}
            className="min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer"
          >
            Batal
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleConfirmResetBook}
            className="min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer"
          >
            Ya, Reset Progres
          </button>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <AuthModal
        open={authActions.authModalOpen}
        isSwitchingAccount={authActions.authSwitchMode}
        busy={authActions.authBusy}
        errorText={authActions.authErrorText}
        infoText={authActions.authInfoText}
        showConsoleHint={authActions.authConsoleHint}
        onClearNotice={authActions.clearAuthNotices}
        onGoogleLogin={authActions.handleGoogleLogin}
        onGithubLogin={authActions.handleGithubLogin}
        onEmailSignIn={authActions.handleEmailSignIn}
        onEmailSignUp={authActions.handleEmailSignUp}
        onEmailReset={authActions.handleEmailReset}
        onClose={() => authActions.setAuthModalOpen(false)}
      />

      <ProfileModal
        open={cloudSync.profileModalOpen}
        initialName={resolvedDisplayName}
        initialAvatarUrl={resolvedAvatarUrl}
        fallbackAuthPhoto={authActions.user?.photoURL ?? ''}
        saving={cloudSync.profileSaving}
        onSave={cloudSync.handleSaveProfile}
        onClose={() => cloudSync.setProfileModalOpen(false)}
      />

      <RestoreModal
        pending={backupRestore.pendingRestore}
        currentMasteredCount={activeMastered.size}
        currentHardCount={activeHardSet.size}
        mergedMasteredCount={backupRestore.restorePreviews.mergedMastered}
        mergedHardCount={backupRestore.restorePreviews.mergedHard}
        cleanBackupHardCount={backupRestore.restorePreviews.cleanImpHard}
        onConfirm={backupRestore.handleConfirmRestore}
        onClose={backupRestore.handleCloseRestoreModal}
      />

      <ToastBanner toast={toast} onDismiss={dismissToast} />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/firebase.ts

import { initializeApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { GithubAuthProvider, GoogleAuthProvider, getAuth } from 'firebase/auth';
import { getFirestore } from 'firebase/firestore';
import firebaseConfig from '../firebase-applet-config.json';

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
export const db = getFirestore(app, firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId);
export const auth = getAuth(app);
export const googleProvider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
export const githubProvider = new GithubAuthProvider();

export function createGoogleAccountPickerProvider(): GoogleAuthProvider {
  const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
  provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });
  return provider;
}

export const FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID = firebaseConfig.projectId;
export const FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID = firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId;
export const FIREBASE_AUTH_PROVIDERS_URL = `https://console.firebase.google.com/project/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/authentication/providers`;

export enum OperationType {
  CREATE = 'create',
  UPDATE = 'update',
  DELETE = 'delete',
  LIST = 'list',
  GET = 'get',
  WRITE = 'write',
}

export interface FirestoreErrorInfo {
  error: string;
  operationType: OperationType;
  path: string | null;
  authInfo: {
    userId?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    emailVerified?: boolean | null;
    isAnonymous?: boolean | null;
    tenantId?: string | null;
    providerInfo?: {
      providerId?: string | null;
      email?: string | null;
    }[];
  };
}

export function isFirestoreQuotaError(error: unknown): boolean {
  const msg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  return (
    msg.includes('Quota exceeded') ||
    msg.includes('resource-exhausted') ||
    msg.includes('RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED')
  );
}

export function getFirestoreUpgradeUrl(): string {
  return `https://console.firebase.google.com/project/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/firestore/databases/${FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID}/data?openUpgradeDialog=true`;
}

export function logFirestoreError(
  error: unknown,
  operationType: OperationType,
  path: string | null
): FirestoreErrorInfo {
  const errInfo: FirestoreErrorInfo = {
    error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    authInfo: {
      userId: auth.currentUser?.uid,
      email: auth.currentUser?.email,
      emailVerified: auth.currentUser?.emailVerified,
      isAnonymous: auth.currentUser?.isAnonymous,
      tenantId: auth.currentUser?.tenantId,
      providerInfo:
        auth.currentUser?.providerData?.map(provider => ({
          providerId: provider.providerId,
          email: provider.email,
        })) || [],
    },
    operationType,
    path,
  };
  console.error('Firestore Error: ', JSON.stringify(errInfo));
  return errInfo;
}

FILE: src/components/AbbreviationsView.tsx

import { BookMarked, CheckCircle2, Layers } from 'lucide-react';
import type { AbbreviationItem, AllBooksProgress, BookData } from '../types/bunpou';
import { FormulaLegend } from './FormulaDisplay';

interface AbbreviationsViewProps {
  abbreviations: AbbreviationItem[];
  books: BookData[];
  booksProgress: AllBooksProgress;
}

export function AbbreviationsView({
  abbreviations,
  books,
  booksProgress,
}: AbbreviationsViewProps) {
  const totalAllPatterns = books.reduce((sum, b) => sum + b.totalPatterns, 0);
  const totalAllChapters = books.reduce((sum, b) => sum + b.chapters.length, 0);
  const totalAllMastered = books.reduce(
    (sum, b) => sum + (booksProgress[b.id]?.mastered.size ?? 0),
    0
  );
  const totalAllHard = books.reduce(
    (sum, b) => sum + (booksProgress[b.id]?.hard.size ?? 0),
    0
  );
  const overallPct =
    totalAllPatterns > 0 ? Math.round((totalAllMastered / totalAllPatterns) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <section aria-label="Panduan singkatan rumus dan ringkasan buku" className="space-y-5">
      {/* Overall Progress Summary across 3 Irodori Books */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <span className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-teal-50 dark:bg-teal-950/60 border border-teal-200 dark:border-teal-800 text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300 inline-flex items-center justify-center">
              <Layers className="w-5 h-5" aria-hidden="true" />
            </span>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                Ringkasan Progres 3 Buku Irodori (A1–A2)
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                {totalAllChapters} Bab tata bahasa · {totalAllPatterns} pola bunpou
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-semibold">
            <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-mono-num">
              {totalAllMastered}/{totalAllPatterns} hafal ({overallPct}%)
            </span>
            <span className="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 font-mono-num">
              {totalAllHard} sulit
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {books.map(book => {
            const bucket = booksProgress[book.id];
            const masteredCount = bucket?.mastered.size ?? 0;
            const hardCount = bucket?.hard.size ?? 0;
            const pct =
              book.totalPatterns > 0 ? Math.round((masteredCount / book.totalPatterns) * 100) : 0;

            return (
              <div
                key={book.id}
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/50 p-3.5 space-y-2"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                    {book.shortTitle}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs font-mono-num font-bold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">
                    {pct}%
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs font-jp text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  {book.japaneseSubtitle} · Bab {book.chapters[0]?.chapterNumber ?? 1}–
                  {book.chapters[book.chapters.length - 1]?.chapterNumber ?? 18} (
                  {book.chapters.length} Bab)
                </p>
                <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className="h-full rounded-full bg-emerald-500 dark:bg-emerald-400 transition-all duration-300"
                    style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono-num">
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-semibold">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {masteredCount}/{book.totalPatterns} hafal
                  </span>
                  <span>{hardCount} sulit</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Abbreviations Guide */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <span className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/60 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 inline-flex items-center justify-center">
            <BookMarked className="w-5 h-5" aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              Daftar Singkatan Rumus Tata Bahasa ({abbreviations.length})
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Panduan membaca simbol kelas kata dan konjugasi pada seluruh buku Irodori.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-slate-700/70 p-3">
          <FormulaLegend />
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
          {abbreviations.map((item, idx) => (
            <div
              key={`${item.code}-${idx}`}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-800/40 px-3.5 py-2.5 flex items-start gap-3"
            >
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-200 font-jp font-bold text-xs shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                {item.code}
              </span>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">
                {item.meaning}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/AppHeader.tsx
