import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from 'react';
import type { User } from 'firebase/auth';
import {
  AlertCircle,
  Camera,
  ChevronDown,
  Cloud,
  Download,
  FolderOpen,
  Languages,
  LogIn,
  LogOut,
  Moon,
  RefreshCw,
  Sun,
  Users,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { BrandLogo } from './BrandLogo';

export interface AppHeaderPreferences {
  showFurigana: boolean;
  onToggleFurigana: () => void;
  showTranslation: boolean;
  onToggleTranslation: () => void;
  isDark: boolean;
  onToggleTheme: () => void;
}

export interface AppHeaderAccount {
  user: User | null;
  displayName: string;
  avatarUrl: string;
  authReady: boolean;
  authBusy: boolean;
  syncing: boolean;
  onOpenAuthModal: (isSwitch: boolean) => void;
  onOpenProfileModal: () => void;
  onManualSync: () => void;
  onResendVerification: () => void;
  onRefreshVerification: () => void;
  onSignOut: () => void;
}

export interface AppHeaderBackup {
  restoreBtnRef: RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>;
  fileInputRef: RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>;
  onExport: () => void;
  onTriggerImport: () => void;
  onFileSelected: (file: File) => void;
}

interface AppHeaderProps {
  bookShortTitle: string;
  masteredCount: number;
  totalCount: number;
  preferences: AppHeaderPreferences;
  account: AppHeaderAccount;
  backup: AppHeaderBackup;
}

function getProviderLabel(user: User): string {
  const providerId = user.providerData?.[0]?.providerId;
  if (providerId === 'google.com') return 'Google';
  if (providerId === 'github.com') return 'GitHub';
  if (providerId === 'password') return 'Email & Sandi';
  return 'Akun Terhubung';
}

export function AppHeader({
  bookShortTitle,
  masteredCount,
  totalCount,
  preferences,
  account,
  backup,
}: AppHeaderProps) {
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [avatarBroken, setAvatarBroken] = useState(false);

  const menuWrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const menuBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  const {
    showFurigana,
    onToggleFurigana,
    showTranslation,
    onToggleTranslation,
    isDark,
    onToggleTheme,
  } = preferences;
  const {
    user,
    displayName,
    avatarUrl,
    authReady,
    authBusy,
    syncing,
    onOpenAuthModal,
    onOpenProfileModal,
    onManualSync,
    onResendVerification,
    onRefreshVerification,
    onSignOut,
  } = account;
  const { restoreBtnRef, fileInputRef, onExport, onTriggerImport, onFileSelected } = backup;

  useEffect(() => {
    setAvatarBroken(false);
  }, [avatarUrl, user?.uid]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) {
      setMenuOpen(false);
    }
  }, [user]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menuOpen) return;

    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (menuWrapperRef.current && !menuWrapperRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setMenuOpen(false);
        requestAnimationFrame(() => menuBtnRef.current?.focus());
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [menuOpen]);

  const resolvedName =
    displayName.trim() || user?.displayName?.trim() || user?.email?.split('@')[0] || 'Pengguna';
  const initial = resolvedName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || 'I';
  const resolvedAvatar = avatarUrl || user?.photoURL || '';
  const isVerified = Boolean(user?.emailVerified);

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white/95 dark:bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 transition-colors">
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-2 lg:py-0 lg:h-16 flex flex-wrap lg:flex-nowrap items-center justify-between gap-x-2 gap-y-2 sm:gap-x-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-2.5 min-w-0 shrink-0 order-1">
          <BrandLogo className="w-8 h-8 sm:w-9 sm:h-9 shrink-0 drop-shadow-xs" />
          <div className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
            <span className="block font-bold text-sm sm:text-base lg:text-lg tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 whitespace-nowrap">
              Irodori Bunpou
            </span>
            <span className="hidden sm:block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap">
              {bookShortTitle}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono-num whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 order-2">
          <strong className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold" id="mCount">
            {masteredCount}
          </strong>
          /<span id="mTotal">{totalCount}</span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline"> hafal</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 shrink-0 order-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnToggleFurigana"
            onClick={onToggleFurigana}
            aria-pressed={showFurigana}
            title={showFurigana ? 'Sembunyikan Furigana' : 'Tampilkan Furigana'}
            className={`min-h-[40px] px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
              showFurigana
                ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/70'
                : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <span className="font-jp font-bold text-sm leading-none" aria-hidden="true">
              あ
            </span>
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">
              Furigana: {showFurigana ? 'Aktif' : 'Mati'}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnToggleTranslation"
            onClick={onToggleTranslation}
            aria-pressed={showTranslation}
            aria-label={showTranslation ? 'Sembunyikan Terjemahan' : 'Tampilkan Terjemahan'}
            title={showTranslation ? 'Sembunyikan Terjemahan' : 'Tampilkan Terjemahan'}
            className={`min-h-[40px] px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
              showTranslation
                ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/70'
                : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Languages className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span className="hidden md:inline">
              Terjemahan: {showTranslation ? 'Aktif' : 'Mati'}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnToggleTheme"
            onClick={onToggleTheme}
            aria-pressed={isDark}
            aria-label={isDark ? 'Aktifkan mode terang' : 'Aktifkan mode gelap'}
            title={isDark ? 'Ganti ke mode terang' : 'Ganti ke mode gelap'}
            className="min-h-[40px] min-w-[40px] px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-amber-300 inline-flex items-center justify-center transition-colors shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
          >
            {isDark ? (
              <Sun className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <Moon className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
          </button>

          <input
            ref={fileInputRef}
            type="file"
            id="fileImportData"
            accept=".json,application/json"
            className="sr-only"
            aria-label="Pilih file backup JSON"
            onChange={e => {
              const file = e.target.files?.[0];
              if (file) onFileSelected(file);
            }}
          />

          {authReady && !user ? (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                id="btnExportData"
                onClick={onExport}
                aria-label="Backup progres (.json)"
                className="min-h-[40px] px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
                title="Backup progres (.json)"
              >
                <Download className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span className="hidden md:inline">Backup</span>
              </button>

              <button
                ref={restoreBtnRef}
                type="button"
                id="btnImportData"
                onClick={onTriggerImport}
                aria-label="Restore progres (.json)"
                className="min-h-[40px] px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
                title="Restore progres (.json)"
              >
                <FolderOpen className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span className="hidden md:inline">Restore</span>
              </button>
            </>
          ) : null}

          {!authReady ? (
            <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="h-10 w-[86px] sm:w-[104px] rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 animate-pulse shrink-0"
            />
          ) : user ? (
            <div ref={menuWrapperRef} className="relative">
              <button
                ref={menuBtnRef}
                type="button"
                id="btnAccountMenu"
                aria-haspopup="menu"
                aria-expanded={menuOpen}
                aria-label={`Menu akun ${resolvedName}`}
                onClick={() => setMenuOpen(o => !o)}
                className="min-h-[40px] px-2 sm:px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 inline-flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
              >
                <span className="relative shrink-0">
                  {resolvedAvatar && !avatarBroken ? (
                    <img
                      src={resolvedAvatar}
                      alt={resolvedName}
                      referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                      onError={() => setAvatarBroken(true)}
                      className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 object-cover bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700"
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-700 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 font-bold text-xs inline-flex items-center justify-center"
                    >
                      {initial}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {!isVerified ? (
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className="absolute -top-0.5 -right-0.5 w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500 border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-800"
                    />
                  ) : syncing ? (
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className="absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-indigo-500 border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-800 animate-pulse"
                    />
                  ) : null}
                </span>

                <span className="hidden md:inline text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 max-w-[130px] truncate">
                  {resolvedName}
                </span>

                <ChevronDown
                  className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 transition-transform shrink-0 ${
                    menuOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                  }`}
                  aria-hidden="true"
                />
              </button>

              {menuOpen ? (
                <div
                  id="accountMenuDropdown"
                  role="menu"
                  aria-labelledby="btnAccountMenu"
                  className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-72 sm:w-80 rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-xl py-2 z-40"
                >
                  <div className="px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-start gap-3">
                    {resolvedAvatar && !avatarBroken ? (
                      <img
                        src={resolvedAvatar}
                        alt={resolvedName}
                        referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                        onError={() => setAvatarBroken(true)}
                        className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 object-cover shrink-0 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <span
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-700 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 font-bold text-sm inline-flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"
                      >
                        {initial}
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 truncate">
                        {resolvedName}
                      </p>
                      {user.email ? (
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">
                          {user.email}
                        </p>
                      ) : null}
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <span>{getProviderLabel(user)}</span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        {isVerified ? (
                          <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Cloud className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                            {syncing ? 'Menyinkronkan...' : 'Cloud aktif'}
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                            <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                            Belum verifikasi email
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {!isVerified ? (
                    <div className="mx-3 my-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/50 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/80 p-2.5 space-y-2">
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 leading-relaxed">
                        Verifikasi email Anda agar progres bisa disinkronkan ke cloud.
                      </p>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={onResendVerification}
                          disabled={authBusy}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-700 text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 hover:bg-amber-100 dark:hover:bg-amber-900/50 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          Kirim Ulang
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={onRefreshVerification}
                          disabled={authBusy}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-600 text-[11px] font-semibold text-white hover:bg-amber-700 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          Cek Status
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : null}

                  <div className="py-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="menuitem"
                      id="menuEditProfile"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenuOpen(false);
                        onOpenProfileModal();
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Camera className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                      <span>Ubah Nama &amp; Foto Profil</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="menuitem"
                      id="menuSwitchAccount"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenuOpen(false);
                        onOpenAuthModal(true);
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Users className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                      <span>Ganti Akun</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="menuitem"
                      id="menuSyncNow"
                      disabled={!isVerified || syncing}
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenuOpen(false);
                        onManualSync();
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-45 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    >
                      <RefreshCw
                        className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 ${
                          syncing ? 'animate-spin' : ''
                        }`}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      />
                      <span>Sinkronkan Progres Sekarang</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="my-1 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800" />

                  <div className="py-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="menuitem"
                      id="menuExportBackup"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenuOpen(false);
                        onExport();
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Download className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                      <span>Backup Progres (.json)</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="menuitem"
                      id="menuImportBackup"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenuOpen(false);
                        onTriggerImport();
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <FolderOpen className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                      <span>Restore dari File (.json)</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="my-1 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800" />

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    role="menuitem"
                    id="btnGoogleLogout"
                    disabled={authBusy}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setMenuOpen(false);
                      onSignOut();
                    }}
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/50 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <LogOut className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>Keluar</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnOpenAuthModal"
              onClick={() => onOpenAuthModal(false)}
              disabled={authBusy}
              aria-label="Masuk ke akun untuk sinkronisasi progres"
              title="Masuk atau daftar akun"
              className="min-h-[40px] px-3 sm:px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
            >
              <LogIn className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Masuk</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/AudioButton.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Volume2, VolumeX } from 'lucide-react';
import { speakJapanese } from '../utils/speech';

interface AudioButtonProps {
  text: string;
  label?: string;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
  className?: string;
}

export function AudioButton({ text, label, size = 'sm', className = '' }: AudioButtonProps) {
  const [speaking, setSpeaking] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const errorTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (errorTimerRef.current !== null) {
        window.clearTimeout(errorTimerRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const handlePlay = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (errorTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(errorTimerRef.current);
      errorTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    setErrorMsg(null);
    speakJapanese(text, {
      onStart: () => setSpeaking(true),
      onEnd: () => setSpeaking(false),
      onError: msg => {
        setSpeaking(false);
        setErrorMsg(msg);
        errorTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
          setErrorMsg(null);
          errorTimerRef.current = null;
        }, 4000);
      },
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="inline-flex flex-col items-end gap-1 shrink-0">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handlePlay}
        title={errorMsg ?? 'Dengarkan pelafalan bahasa Jepang'}
        aria-label={label ? `Putar audio: ${label}` : 'Putar pelafalan bahasa Jepang'}
        className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border transition-colors shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
          size === 'md'
            ? 'min-h-[40px] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold'
            : 'min-h-[34px] px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold'
        } ${
          errorMsg
            ? 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/60 border-rose-300 dark:border-rose-800 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-200'
            : speaking
              ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 text-white'
              : 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/70'
        } ${className}`}
      >
        {errorMsg ? (
          <VolumeX className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
        ) : (
          <Volume2
            className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 ${speaking ? 'animate-pulse' : ''}`}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
        )}
        {label ? <span>{errorMsg ? 'Audio gagal' : label}</span> : null}
      </button>
      {errorMsg ? (
        <span
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          className="text-[11px] text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300 max-w-[200px] text-right leading-tight"
        >
          {errorMsg}
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Eye, EyeOff, KeyRound, Mail, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { FIREBASE_AUTH_PROVIDERS_URL } from '../firebase';
import { Modal } from './Modal';

export type AuthModalView = 'signin' | 'signup' | 'reset';

interface AuthModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  isSwitchingAccount: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
  errorText: string | null;
  infoText: string | null;
  showConsoleHint: boolean;
  onClearNotice: () => void;
  onGoogleLogin: (forceSelectAccount: boolean) => void;
  onGithubLogin: () => void;
  onEmailSignIn: (email: string, password: string) => void;
  onEmailSignUp: (name: string, email: string, password: string) => void;
  onEmailReset: (email: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

function GoogleGlyph() {
  return (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
      <path
        fill="#4285F4"
        d="M23.745 12.27c0-.7-.06-1.4-.19-2.07H12v4.51h6.6c-.29 1.52-1.14 2.82-2.4 3.68v3.05h3.88c2.27-2.09 3.665-5.17 3.665-9.17Z"
      />
      <path
        fill="#34A853"
        d="M12 24c3.24 0 5.95-1.08 7.93-2.91l-3.88-3.05c-1.08.72-2.45 1.16-4.05 1.16-3.12 0-5.77-2.11-6.72-4.96H1.29v3.14C3.26 21.3 7.31 24 12 24Z"
      />
      <path
        fill="#FBBC05"
        d="M5.28 14.24c-.24-.72-.38-1.49-.38-2.24s.14-1.52.38-2.24V6.62H1.29C.47 8.24 0 10.06 0 12s.47 3.76 1.29 5.38l3.99-3.14Z"
      />
      <path
        fill="#EA4335"
        d="M12 4.75c1.77 0 3.35.61 4.6 1.8l3.42-3.42C17.95 1.19 15.24 0 12 0 7.31 0 3.26 2.7 1.29 6.62l3.99 3.14c.95-2.85 3.6-4.96 6.72-4.96Z"
      />
    </svg>
  );
}

function GithubGlyph() {
  return (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 fill-current" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M12 0C5.37 0 0 5.37 0 12c0 5.31 3.435 9.795 8.205 11.385.6.105.825-.255.825-.57 0-.285-.015-1.23-.015-2.235-3.015.555-3.795-.735-4.035-1.41-.135-.345-.72-1.41-1.23-1.695-.42-.225-1.02-.78-.015-.795.945-.015 1.62.87 1.845 1.23 1.08 1.815 2.805 1.305 3.495.99.105-.78.42-1.305.765-1.605-2.67-.3-5.46-1.335-5.46-5.925 0-1.305.465-2.385 1.23-3.225-.12-.3-.54-1.53.12-3.18 0 0 1.005-.315 3.3 1.23.96-.27 1.98-.405 3-.405s2.04.135 3 .405c2.295-1.56 3.3-1.23 3.3-1.23.66 1.65.24 2.88.12 3.18.765.84 1.23 1.905 1.23 3.225 0 4.605-2.805 5.625-5.475 5.925.435.375.81 1.095.81 2.22 0 1.605-.015 2.895-.015 3.3 0 .315.225.69.825.57A12.02 12.02 0 0 0 24 12c0-6.63-5.37-12-12-12Z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function AuthModal({
  open,
  isSwitchingAccount,
  busy,
  errorText,
  infoText,
  showConsoleHint,
  onClearNotice,
  onGoogleLogin,
  onGithubLogin,
  onEmailSignIn,
  onEmailSignUp,
  onEmailReset,
  onClose,
}: AuthModalProps) {
  const [view, setView] = useState<AuthModalView>('signin');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPass, setShowPass] = useState(false);

  const closeBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setView('signin');
    setPassword('');
    setShowPass(false);
  }, [open]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const switchView = (next: AuthModalView) => {
    setView(next);
    onClearNotice();
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (view === 'signin') {
      onEmailSignIn(email, password);
    } else if (view === 'signup') {
      onEmailSignUp(name, email, password);
    } else {
      onEmailReset(email);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      open={open}
      onClose={onClose}
      id="authModalOverlay"
      ariaLabelledBy="authModalTitle"
      initialFocusRef={closeBtnRef}
      panelClassName="overflow-hidden"
    >
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h2 id="authModalTitle" className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              {isSwitchingAccount ? 'Ganti Akun Belajar' : 'Masuk & Sinkronisasi Progres'}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              {isSwitchingAccount
                ? 'Pilih akun lain untuk melanjutkan atau berpindah progres.'
                : 'Simpan progres belajar Irodori Bunpou (A1–A2) ke database cloud khusus aplikasi ini.'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            ref={closeBtnRef}
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Tutup dialog login"
            className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-50 inline-flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer shrink-0"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnAuthGoogle"
              disabled={busy}
              onClick={() => onGoogleLogin(isSwitchingAccount)}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <GoogleGlyph />
              <span>{isSwitchingAccount ? 'Pilih Akun Google' : 'Google'}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnAuthGithub"
              disabled={busy}
              onClick={onGithubLogin}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-600 bg-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <GithubGlyph />
              <span>GitHub</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="relative flex items-center py-0.5">
            <div className="grow border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700" />
            <span className="shrink mx-3 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
              atau dengan email
            </span>
            <div className="grow border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700" />
          </div>

          <div
            role="tablist"
            aria-label="Mode autentikasi email"
            className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 p-1"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={view === 'signin'}
              onClick={() => switchView('signin')}
              className={`min-h-[34px] rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                view === 'signin'
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              Masuk
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={view === 'signup'}
              onClick={() => switchView('signup')}
              className={`min-h-[34px] rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                view === 'signup'
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              Daftar Baru
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={view === 'reset'}
              onClick={() => switchView('reset')}
              className={`min-h-[34px] rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                view === 'reset'
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              Lupa Sandi
            </button>
          </div>

          {errorText ? (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/50 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-200 space-y-1.5"
            >
              <p className="font-semibold">{errorText}</p>
              {showConsoleHint ? (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300 leading-relaxed">
                  Aktifkan metode login ini di{' '}
                  <a
                    href={FIREBASE_AUTH_PROVIDERS_URL}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                    className="underline font-semibold"
                  >
                    Firebase Console → Authentication → Sign-in method
                  </a>
                  .
                </p>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {infoText ? (
            <div
              role="status"
              className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/80 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-200 font-medium"
            >
              {infoText}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
            {view === 'signup' ? (
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="authNameInput"
                  className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-1"
                >
                  Nama Tampilan
                </label>
                <input
                  id="authNameInput"
                  type="text"
                  maxLength={60}
                  value={name}
                  onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Contoh: Budi Santoso"
                  className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
                />
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <div>
              <label
                htmlFor="authEmailInput"
                className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-1"
              >
                Alamat Email
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail
                  className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                />
                <input
                  id="authEmailInput"
                  type="email"
                  required
                  autoComplete="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="nama@contoh.com"
                  className="w-full h-10 pl-9 pr-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {view !== 'reset' ? (
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="authPasswordInput"
                  className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-1"
                >
                  Kata Sandi
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <KeyRound
                    className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                  <input
                    id="authPasswordInput"
                    type={showPass ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    required
                    minLength={6}
                    autoComplete={view === 'signup' ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'}
                    value={password}
                    onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Minimal 6 karakter"
                    className="w-full h-10 pl-9 pr-10 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPass(s => !s)}
                    aria-label={showPass ? 'Sembunyikan sandi' : 'Lihat sandi'}
                    className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1.5 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {showPass ? (
                      <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    ) : (
                      <Eye className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={busy}
              className="w-full min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {busy
                ? 'Memproses...'
                : view === 'signin'
                  ? 'Masuk dengan Email'
                  : view === 'signup'
                    ? 'Buat Akun & Kirim Verifikasi'
                    : 'Kirim Link Reset Sandi'}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

      />

        d="M46 31.5C46 29.8 44.7 28.5 43 28.5H35C31.7 28.5 29 31.2 29 34.5V49C31.2 47.4 33.8 46.5 36.7 46.5H43C44.7 46.5 46 45.2 46 43.5V31.5Z"
        fill="#e0e7ff"
      />

      <path
        d="M36 16.5C41.5 14 47.2 13 52 13.2"
        stroke="#34d399"
        strokeWidth="5.2"
        strokeLinecap="round"
      />
      <path
        d="M38.5 26C44 23.8 48.8 23 53 23.4"
        stroke="#fbbf24"
        strokeWidth="5.2"
        strokeLinecap="round"
      />
      <path
        d="M49.5 34.5C51.2 34.2 52.8 34.1 54 34.3"
        stroke="#38bdf8"
        strokeWidth="5.2"

      />

================================================
FILE: src/components/BunpouListPanel.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Search,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { BunpouPattern } from '../types/bunpou';
import { extractFuriganaReading, stripFuriganaMarkup } from '../utils/furiganaConverter';
import { AudioButton } from './AudioButton';
import { FormulaDisplay, FormulaLegend } from './FormulaDisplay';
import { JapaneseText } from './JapaneseText';

type ListStatusFilter = 'all' | 'unmastered' | 'mastered' | 'hard';

interface BunpouListPanelProps {
  patterns: BunpouPattern[];
  showFurigana: boolean;
  showTranslation: boolean;
  masteredSet: Set<string>;
  hardSet: Set<string>;
  bookShortTitle: string;
  chapterLabel: string;
  onToggleMastered: (patternId: string) => void;
  onToggleHard: (patternId: string) => void;
}

export function BunpouListPanel({
  patterns,
  showFurigana,
  showTranslation,
  masteredSet,
  hardSet,
  bookShortTitle,
  chapterLabel,
  onToggleMastered,
  onToggleHard,
}: BunpouListPanelProps) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<ListStatusFilter>('all');
  const [expandedIds, setExpandedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

  useEffect(() => {
    setQuery('');
    setStatusFilter('all');
    setExpandedIds(new Set());
  }, [bookShortTitle]);

  const indexedPatterns = useMemo(
    () =>
      patterns.map(p => ({
        pattern: p,
        haystack: [
          p.title,
          p.shortPattern,
          p.itemCode ?? '',
          p.topicJp ?? '',
          p.topicId ?? '',
          p.marugotoRef ?? '',
          p.functionText,
          p.chapterTitleId,
          p.chapterTitleJp,
          `bab ${p.chapterNumber}`,
          `第${p.chapterNumber}課`,
          ...p.formulas,
          ...p.notes,
          ...p.examples.map(
            e =>
              `${stripFuriganaMarkup(e.japanese)} ${extractFuriganaReading(e.japanese)} ${e.translation}`
          ),
        ]
          .join(' ')
          .toLowerCase(),
      })),
    [patterns]
  );

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return indexedPatterns
      .filter(({ pattern: p, haystack }) => {
        if (statusFilter === 'mastered' && !masteredSet.has(p.id)) return false;
        if (statusFilter === 'hard' && !hardSet.has(p.id)) return false;
        if (statusFilter === 'unmastered' && masteredSet.has(p.id)) return false;
        if (!q) return true;
        return haystack.includes(q);
      })
      .map(item => item.pattern);
  }, [indexedPatterns, query, statusFilter, masteredSet, hardSet]);

  const toggleExpand = (id: string) => {
    setExpandedIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
      else next.add(id);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const allExpanded =
    filtered.length > 0 && filtered.every(p => expandedIds.has(p.id));

  const handleToggleAll = () => {
    if (allExpanded) {
      setExpandedIds(new Set());
    } else {
      setExpandedIds(new Set(filtered.map(p => p.id)));
    }
  };

  return (
    <section aria-label="Daftar pola tata bahasa Irodori" className="space-y-4">
      {/* Search + Filters Card */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm space-y-3">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <Search
              className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <label htmlFor="bunpouSearchInput" className="sr-only">
              Cari pola bunpou, rumus, fungsi, atau contoh kalimat
            </label>
            <input
              id="bunpouSearchInput"
              type="text"
              value={query}
              onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Cari pola, rumus, fungsi, atau contoh (mis. たいです, izin, Bab 5)..."
              className="w-full min-h-[42px] pl-10 pr-9 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
            />
            {query ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setQuery('')}
                aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
                className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              </button>
            ) : null}
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleToggleAll}
            disabled={filtered.length === 0}
            className="min-h-[42px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-45 shrink-0"
          >
            {allExpanded ? (
              <>
                <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Tutup Semua</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Buka Semua</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5" role="group" aria-label="Filter status">
            {(
              [
                { id: 'all', label: 'Semua' },
                { id: 'unmastered', label: 'Belum Hafal' },
                { id: 'mastered', label: 'Sudah Hafal' },
                { id: 'hard', label: 'Perlu Review' },
              ] as const
            ).map(opt => {
              const active = statusFilter === opt.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={opt.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setStatusFilter(opt.id)}
                  aria-pressed={active}
                  className={`min-h-[34px] px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                    active
                      ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                      : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {opt.label}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">
            Menampilkan{' '}
            <strong className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 font-mono-num">
              {filtered.length}
            </strong>{' '}
            dari{' '}
            <span className="font-mono-num">{patterns.length}</span> pola ({bookShortTitle} ·{' '}
            {chapterLabel})
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
          <FormulaLegend />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Results List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center shadow-sm space-y-2">
          <p className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
            Tidak ada pola yang cocok
          </p>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            Coba ubah kata kunci pencarian atau reset filter status.
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setQuery('');
              setStatusFilter('all');
            }}
            className="mt-2 min-h-[38px] px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
          >
            Reset Filter
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          {filtered.map(pattern => {
            const isMastered = masteredSet.has(pattern.id);
            const isHard = hardSet.has(pattern.id);
            const isOpen = expandedIds.has(pattern.id);

            return (
              <article
                key={pattern.id}
                className={`bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border rounded-2xl transition-colors shadow-sm ${
                  isMastered
                    ? 'border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/70'
                    : isHard
                      ? 'border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/70'
                      : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleExpand(pattern.id)}
                      aria-expanded={isOpen}
                      className="text-left flex-1 min-w-0 group cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 rounded-lg"
                    >
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-[11px] font-bold">
                          Bab {pattern.chapterNumber} · #{pattern.itemCode ?? pattern.patternIndex}
                        </span>
                        {pattern.topicJp ? (
                          <span className="font-jp px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-50/70 dark:bg-indigo-950/50 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 text-[11px] font-semibold truncate max-w-[160px]">
                            {pattern.topicJp}
                          </span>
                        ) : null}
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate max-w-[240px]">
                          {pattern.chapterTitleJp} · {pattern.chapterTitleId}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <h3 className="font-jp text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 group-hover:text-indigo-700 dark:group-hover:text-indigo-300 transition-colors">
                        {pattern.title}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mt-1 line-clamp-2">
                        {pattern.functionText}
                      </p>
                    </button>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onToggleHard(pattern.id)}
                        aria-pressed={isHard}
                        title={isHard ? 'Hapus tanda Sulit' : 'Tandai Perlu Review'}
                        className={`min-h-[36px] min-w-[36px] px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                          isHard
                            ? 'bg-amber-500 border-amber-500 text-white'
                            : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:border-amber-300 hover:text-amber-700 dark:hover:text-amber-300'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                        <span className="hidden sm:inline">Sulit</span>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onToggleMastered(pattern.id)}
                        aria-pressed={isMastered}
                        title={isMastered ? 'Batalkan status Hafal' : 'Tandai Sudah Hafal'}
                        className={`min-h-[36px] min-w-[36px] px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                          isMastered
                            ? 'bg-emerald-600 border-emerald-600 text-white'
                            : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:border-emerald-300 hover:text-emerald-700 dark:hover:text-emerald-300'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                        <span className="hidden sm:inline">Hafal</span>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => toggleExpand(pattern.id)}
                        aria-expanded={isOpen}
                        aria-label={isOpen ? 'Tutup detail pola' : 'Buka detail pola'}
                        className="min-h-[36px] min-w-[36px] p-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 inline-flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        {isOpen ? (
                          <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                        ) : (
                          <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                        )}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Compact Formula Preview always visible */}
                  <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/70 dark:border-slate-700/60 px-3 py-2">
                    <FormulaDisplay formulas={pattern.formulas} compact />
                  </div>

                  {/* Expanded Detail: Notes + Examples with Furigana & Audio */}
                  {isOpen ? (
                    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-3">
                      {pattern.notes.length > 0 ? (
                        <div className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-50/60 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 border border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-900/60 p-3">
                          <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 mb-1">
                            Catatan Penting
                          </p>
                          <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 list-disc pl-4">
                            {pattern.notes.map((note, idx) => (
                              <li key={idx}>{note}</li>
                            ))}
                          </ul>
                        </div>
                      ) : null}

                      <div className="space-y-2">
                        <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                          Contoh Kalimat ({pattern.examples.length})
                        </p>
                        {pattern.examples.map((ex, idx) => (
                          <div
                            key={idx}
                            className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-slate-700/70 p-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-3"
                          >
                            <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1">
                              <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 font-medium">
                                <JapaneseText text={ex.japanese} showFurigana={showFurigana} />
                              </p>
                              {showTranslation && ex.translation ? (
                                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                                  {ex.translation}
                                </p>
                              ) : null}
                            </div>
                            <AudioButton text={ex.japanese} label="Dengar" />
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : null}
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
================================================
import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { getFirestoreUpgradeUrl, isFirestoreQuotaError } from '../firebase';

interface ErrorBoundaryProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

interface ErrorBoundaryState {
  error: Error | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<ErrorBoundaryProps, ErrorBoundaryState> {
  state: ErrorBoundaryState = { error: null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): ErrorBoundaryState {
    return { error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo): void {
    console.error('Application ErrorBoundary caught:', error, info);
  }

  handleRetry = () => {
    this.setState({ error: null });
  };

  render() {
    const { error } = this.state;
    if (!error) {
      return this.props.children;
    }

    const rawMsg = error.message || 'Terjadi kesalahan pada aplikasi.';
    const isQuotaExceeded = isFirestoreQuotaError(error);
    const isFreeDailyReadLimit =
      rawMsg.includes('Free daily read units per project') || isQuotaExceeded;

    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 shadow-lg p-6 space-y-4"
        >
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <h1 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              {isQuotaExceeded
                ? 'Batas Kuota Firestore Tercapai'
                : 'Terjadi Gangguan Aplikasi'}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
              {isQuotaExceeded
                ? 'Kuota harian gratis Firestore telah habis. Anda tetap dapat menggunakan seluruh 52 bab dan 264 pola secara lokal di perangkat ini.'
                : 'Aplikasi mengalami kendala tak terduga. Coba pulihkan tampilan atau muat ulang halaman.'}
            </p>
          </div>

          {isFreeDailyReadLimit ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/50 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/80 p-3 text-xs text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 break-all">
              Kelola kuota database:{' '}
              <a
                href={getFirestoreUpgradeUrl()}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="underline font-semibold"
              >
                {getFirestoreUpgradeUrl()}
              </a>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 pt-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={this.handleRetry}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Coba Lagi
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Muat Ulang Halaman
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
}

FILE: src/components/FlashcardPanel.tsx
