import {
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  RotateCcw,
  Shuffle,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { BunpouPattern, DeckStatusFilter } from '../types/bunpou';
import { AudioButton } from './AudioButton';
import { FormulaDisplay } from './FormulaDisplay';
import { JapaneseText } from './JapaneseText';

interface FlashcardPanelProps {
  deck: BunpouPattern[];
  currentIndex: number;
  isFlipped: boolean;
  showFurigana: boolean;
  showTranslation: boolean;
  deckFilter: DeckStatusFilter;
  masteredSet: Set<string>;
  hardSet: Set<string>;
  bookShortTitle: string;
  chapterLabel: string;
  onChangeDeckFilter: (filter: DeckStatusFilter) => void;
  onFlip: () => void;
  onPrev: () => void;
  onNext: () => void;
  onShuffle: () => void;
  onMarkMastered: (patternId: string) => void;
  onMarkHard: (patternId: string) => void;
  onResetBookProgress: () => void;
}

export function FlashcardPanel({
  deck,
  currentIndex,
  isFlipped,
  showFurigana,
  showTranslation,
  deckFilter,
  masteredSet,
  hardSet,
  bookShortTitle,
  chapterLabel,
  onChangeDeckFilter,
  onFlip,
  onPrev,
  onNext,
  onShuffle,
  onMarkMastered,
  onMarkHard,
  onResetBookProgress,
}: FlashcardPanelProps) {
  const safeIdx = deck.length > 0 ? Math.min(currentIndex, deck.length - 1) : 0;
  const current = deck[safeIdx] ?? null;
  const isMastered = current ? masteredSet.has(current.id) : false;
  const isHard = current ? hardSet.has(current.id) : false;

  return (
    <section aria-label="Kartu hafalan bunpou" className="space-y-4">
      {/* Top Toolbar */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5" role="group" aria-label="Filter kartu">
          {(
            [
              { id: 'all', label: 'Semua' },
              { id: 'unmastered', label: 'Belum Hafal' },
              { id: 'hard', label: 'Tanda Sulit' },
            ] as const
          ).map(opt => {
            const active = deckFilter === opt.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onChangeDeckFilter(opt.id)}
                aria-pressed={active}
                className={`min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                {opt.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 ml-auto">
          <span
            id="cardCounter"
            className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-mono-num font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 px-2"
          >
            {deck.length > 0 ? `${safeIdx + 1} / ${deck.length}` : '0 / 0'}
          </span>

          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnShuffleDeck"
            onClick={onShuffle}
            disabled={deck.length < 2}
            className="min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-45"
          >
            <Shuffle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>Acak</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnResetProgress"
            onClick={onResetBookProgress}
            title={`Reset progres ${bookShortTitle}`}
            className="min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/50 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-700 dark:hover:text-rose-300 hover:border-rose-200 dark:hover:border-rose-800 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Reset</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Empty State */}
      {!current ? (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 sm:p-12 text-center shadow-sm space-y-3">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 inline-flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
            <Sparkles className="w-6 h-6" aria-hidden="true" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
            {deckFilter === 'hard'
              ? 'Tidak ada pola bertanda Sulit di cakupan ini'
              : deckFilter === 'unmastered'
                ? 'Semua pola di cakupan ini sudah Hafal!'
                : 'Belum ada kartu untuk cakupan ini'}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto">
            {chapterLabel} · {bookShortTitle}. Anda bisa menampilkan kembali semua kartu untuk
            mengulang latihan.
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChangeDeckFilter('all')}
            className="mt-2 min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            Tampilkan Semua Kartu
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* 3D Flip Card */}
          <div className="perspective-1200">
            <div
              id="flashcardContainer"
              role="button"
              tabIndex={0}
              aria-pressed={isFlipped}
              aria-label={
                isFlipped
                  ? `Sisi belakang kartu ${current.title}. Klik untuk membalik ke depan.`
                  : `Sisi depan kartu ${current.title}. Klik untuk melihat rumus dan contoh.`
              }
              onClick={onFlip}
              onKeyDown={e => {
                if (e.target !== e.currentTarget) return;
                if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onFlip();
                }
              }}
              className={`card-flip-inner relative w-full min-h-[380px] sm:min-h-[420px] cursor-pointer select-none focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 rounded-3xl ${
                isFlipped ? 'is-flipped' : ''
              }`}
            >
              {/* FRONT FACE */}
              <div className="card-face absolute inset-0 rounded-3xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm p-5 sm:p-8 flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs font-bold">
                      {current.bookShortTitle} · Bab {current.chapterNumber}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium truncate max-w-[220px] sm:max-w-[320px]">
                      {current.chapterTitleId}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {isMastered ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/70 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 text-xs font-bold">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      Sudah Hafal
                    </span>
                  ) : isHard ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/70 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold">
                      <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      Perlu Review
                    </span>
                  ) : null}
                </div>

                <div className="my-auto py-6 text-center space-y-3">
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">
                    Pola Tata Bahasa #{current.itemCode ?? current.patternIndex}
                    {current.topicJp ? ` · ${current.topicJp}` : ''}
                  </p>
                  <h2
                    id="flashcardFrontTitle"
                    className="font-jp text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 leading-snug break-words"
                  >
                    {current.shortPattern}
                  </h2>
                  {current.title !== current.shortPattern ? (
                    <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 max-w-xl mx-auto">
                      {current.title}
                    </p>
                  ) : null}
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  <span className="font-jp truncate">{current.chapterTitleJp}</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shrink-0">
                    Klik / Spasi untuk balik →
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* BACK FACE */}
              <div className="card-face card-face-back absolute inset-0 rounded-3xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-2 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800/80 shadow-sm p-5 sm:p-7 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">
                      Bab {current.chapterNumber} · Rumus &amp; Fungsi
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="font-jp text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 truncate">
                      {current.title}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-medium shrink-0">
                    Klik untuk tutup
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="py-4 space-y-4 flex-1">
                  {/* Formula */}
                  <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/70 border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-slate-700/70 p-3.5">
                    <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                      Rumus Penyambungan
                    </p>
                    <FormulaDisplay formulas={current.formulas} />
                  </div>

                  {/* Function */}
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">
                      Fungsi &amp; Makna
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-sm sm:text-[15px] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 leading-relaxed font-medium">
                      {current.functionText}
                    </p>
                    {current.notes.length > 0 ? (
                      <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 list-disc pl-4">
                        {current.notes.map((note, idx) => (
                          <li key={idx}>{note}</li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    ) : null}
                  </div>

                  {/* Examples */}
                  {current.examples.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Contoh Kalimat ({current.examples.length})
                      </p>
                      <div className="space-y-2">
                        {current.examples.map((ex, idx) => (
                          <div
                            key={idx}
                            onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
                            className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-slate-700/70 p-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-3"
                          >
                            <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1">
                              <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 font-medium">
                                <JapaneseText text={ex.japanese} showFurigana={showFurigana} />
                              </p>
                              {showTranslation && ex.translation ? (
                                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                                  {ex.translation}
                                </p>
                              ) : null}
                            </div>
                            <AudioButton text={ex.japanese} label="Dengar" />
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : null}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Action Controls */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-[auto_1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2.5 items-center">
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnPrevCard"
              onClick={onPrev}
              disabled={deck.length < 2}
              aria-label="Kartu sebelumnya"
              className="min-h-[46px] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-semibold text-sm inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-45 order-3 sm:order-1"
            >
              <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Sebelumnya</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnMarkHard"
              onClick={() => onMarkHard(current.id)}
              aria-pressed={isHard}
              className={`min-h-[46px] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border text-sm font-bold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer order-1 sm:order-2 ${
                isHard
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 border-amber-500 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/50 border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200 hover:bg-amber-100 dark:hover:bg-amber-900/60'
              }`}
            >
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>{isHard ? 'Ditandai Sulit' : 'Ulangi / Sulit'}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnMarkMastered"
              onClick={() => onMarkMastered(current.id)}
              aria-pressed={isMastered}
              className={`min-h-[46px] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border text-sm font-bold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer order-2 sm:order-3 ${
                isMastered
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 border-emerald-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-100 dark:hover:bg-emerald-900/60'
              }`}
            >
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>{isMastered ? 'Sudah Hafal' : 'Tandai Hafal'}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnNextCard"
              onClick={onNext}
              disabled={deck.length < 2}
              aria-label="Kartu berikutnya"
              className="min-h-[46px] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-semibold text-sm inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-45 order-4"
            >
              <span>Berikutnya</span>
              <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <p className="hidden sm:block text-center text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
            Pintasan keyboard: <kbd className="font-mono-num px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">Spasi</kbd> Balik ·{' '}
            <kbd className="font-mono-num px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">←</kbd>{' '}
            <kbd className="font-mono-num px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">→</kbd> Pindah ·{' '}
            <kbd className="font-mono-num px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">H</kbd> Hafal ·{' '}
            <kbd className="font-mono-num px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">U</kbd> Sulit
          </p>
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

import { memo, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { FormulaTokenKind } from '../types/bunpou';
import { FORMULA_LEGEND, tokenizeFormula } from '../utils/formulaTokenizer';

const TOKEN_STYLES: Record<FormulaTokenKind, string> = {
  verb: 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-200 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg font-jp',
  noun: 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/60 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg font-jp',
  adj: 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-200 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg font-jp',
  particle:
    'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/60 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-200 border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg font-jp',
  plain:
    'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg',
  operator: 'text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-mono-num text-xs px-0.5 select-none',
  note: 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-xs italic',
  text: 'text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 font-jp font-semibold',
};

interface FormulaDisplayProps {
  formulas: string[];
  compact?: boolean;
  centered?: boolean;
}

export const FormulaDisplay = memo(function FormulaDisplay({
  formulas,
  compact = false,
  centered = false,
}: FormulaDisplayProps) {
  const tokenizedLines = useMemo(() => formulas.map(line => tokenizeFormula(line)), [formulas]);

  return (
    <div className={compact ? 'space-y-1.5' : 'space-y-2'}>
      {tokenizedLines.map((tokens, lineIdx) => (
        <div
          key={lineIdx}
          className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 ${
            centered ? 'justify-center' : ''
          } ${compact ? 'text-xs sm:text-[13px]' : 'text-sm'} leading-relaxed`}
        >
          {tokens.map((tok, tokIdx) => (
            <span key={tokIdx} className={TOKEN_STYLES[tok.kind]}>
              {tok.text}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
});

export function FormulaLegend() {
  return (
    <div
      className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300"
      aria-label="Legenda warna rumus tata bahasa"
    >
      <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Kode warna:</span>
      {FORMULA_LEGEND.map(item => (
        <span key={item.kind} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className={`${TOKEN_STYLES[item.kind]} text-[11px] px-1.5 py-0`}>
            {item.example}
          </span>
          <span>{item.label}</span>
        </span>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/IrodoriBookMenu.tsx

import { BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import type { AllBooksProgress, BookData, BookId } from '../types/bunpou';

interface IrodoriBookMenuProps {
  books: BookData[];
  activeBookId: BookId;
  booksProgress: AllBooksProgress;
  onSelectBook: (bookId: BookId) => void;
}

const BOOK_ACCENTS: Record<BookId, { badge: string; activeRing: string; bar: string }> = {
  introductory: {
    badge: 'bg-teal-100 dark:bg-teal-900/60 text-teal-800 dark:text-teal-200',
    activeRing:
      'bg-teal-50 dark:bg-teal-950/60 border-teal-500 dark:border-teal-500/80 text-teal-950 dark:text-teal-50 shadow-sm',
    bar: 'bg-teal-500 dark:bg-teal-400',
  },
  elementary1: {
    badge: 'bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-900/60 text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-200',
    activeRing:
      'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/60 border-indigo-500 dark:border-indigo-500/80 text-indigo-950 dark:text-indigo-50 shadow-sm',
    bar: 'bg-indigo-500 dark:bg-indigo-400',
  },
  elementary2: {
    badge: 'bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-900/60 text-violet-800 dark:text-violet-200',
    activeRing:
      'bg-violet-50 dark:bg-violet-950/60 border-violet-500 dark:border-violet-500/80 text-violet-950 dark:text-violet-50 shadow-sm',
    bar: 'bg-violet-500 dark:bg-violet-400',
  },
};

export function IrodoriBookMenu({
  books,
  activeBookId,
  booksProgress,
  onSelectBook,
}: IrodoriBookMenuProps) {
  return (
    <section
      aria-label="Pilih buku Irodori"
      className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-2 sm:p-2.5 shadow-sm"
    >
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
        {books.map(book => {
          const isActive = book.id === activeBookId;
          const bucket = booksProgress[book.id];
          const masteredCount = bucket?.mastered.size ?? 0;
          const total = book.totalPatterns;
          const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((masteredCount / total) * 100) : 0;
          const accent = BOOK_ACCENTS[book.id];

          return (
            <button
              key={book.id}
              type="button"
              id={`btnBook-${book.id}`}
              aria-pressed={isActive}
              onClick={() => onSelectBook(book.id)}
              className={`w-full text-left rounded-xl border px-3.5 py-2.5 transition-all cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                isActive
                  ? accent.activeRing
                  : 'bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100/80 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                  <BookOpen
                    className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 ${
                      isActive
                        ? 'text-current'
                        : 'text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500'
                    }`}
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                  <span className="font-bold text-sm truncate">{book.shortTitle}</span>
                </div>
                <span
                  className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold shrink-0 ${accent.badge}`}
                >
                  {book.level}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                <span className="font-jp truncate">{book.japaneseSubtitle}</span>
                <span className="font-mono-num font-semibold shrink-0 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                  {masteredCount}/{total}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-200/80 dark:bg-slate-700/80 overflow-hidden">
                <div
                  className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${accent.bar}`}
                  style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/JapaneseText.tsx

import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { buildRubySegments } from '../utils/furiganaConverter';

interface JapaneseTextProps {
  text: string;
  showFurigana: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

export function JapaneseText({ text, showFurigana, className = '' }: JapaneseTextProps) {
  const segments = useMemo(() => buildRubySegments(text), [text]);

  return (
    <span
      lang="ja"
      className={`font-jp ${showFurigana ? 'leading-[2.15]' : 'leading-relaxed'} ${className}`}
    >
      {segments.map((seg, idx) =>
        seg.reading ? (
          <ruby key={idx}>
            {seg.text}
            {showFurigana ? (
              <>
                <rp>(</rp>
                <rt>{seg.reading}</rt>
                <rp>)</rp>
              </>
            ) : null}
          </ruby>
        ) : (
          <span key={idx}>{seg.text}</span>
        )
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/Modal.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode, type RefObject } from 'react';

interface ModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  id?: string;
  ariaLabelledBy: string;
  ariaDescribedBy?: string;
  maxWidthClass?: string;
  panelClassName?: string;
  initialFocusRef?: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
  children: ReactNode;
}

const FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR =
  'button:not([disabled]), [href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';

export function Modal({
  open,
  onClose,
  id,
  ariaLabelledBy,
  ariaDescribedBy,
  maxWidthClass = 'max-w-md',
  panelClassName = '',
  initialFocusRef,
  children,
}: ModalProps) {
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose);
  const initialFocusRefLatest = useRef(initialFocusRef);

  onCloseRef.current = onClose;
  initialFocusRefLatest.current = initialFocusRef;

  // Depend ONLY on [open] so parent re-renders with new inline onClose callbacks never steal focus
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;

    const focusTimer = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      const target = initialFocusRefLatest.current?.current;
      if (target) {
        target.focus();
        return;
      }
      const firstFocusable = panelRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR);
      firstFocusable?.focus();
    });

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        onCloseRef.current();
        return;
      }

      if (e.key !== 'Tab' || !panelRef.current) return;
      const focusables = Array.from(
        panelRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR)
      ).filter(el => el.offsetParent !== null);
      if (focusables.length === 0) return;

      const first = focusables[0];
      const last = focusables[focusables.length - 1];
      const active = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;

      if (e.shiftKey) {
        if (active === first || !panelRef.current.contains(active)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          last.focus();
        }
      } else if (active === last || !panelRef.current.contains(active)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(focusTimer);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);
    };
  }, [open]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div
      id={id}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby={ariaLabelledBy}
      aria-describedby={ariaDescribedBy}
      onClick={e => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onCloseRef.current();
      }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/55 dark:bg-slate-950/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
    >
      <div
        ref={panelRef}
        className={`w-full ${maxWidthClass} rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-2xl ${panelClassName}`}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ProfileModal.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Camera, RotateCcw, Upload, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { PRESET_AVATARS, compressAvatarFile, renderPresetKanjiAvatar } from '../utils/avatarImage';
import { DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH, isValidAvatarUrl } from '../utils/bunpouSync';
import { Modal } from './Modal';

interface ProfileModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  initialName: string;
  initialAvatarUrl: string;
  fallbackAuthPhoto: string;
  saving: boolean;
  onSave: (displayName: string, avatarUrl: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ProfileModal({
  open,
  initialName,
  initialAvatarUrl,
  fallbackAuthPhoto,
  saving,
  onSave,
  onClose,
}: ProfileModalProps) {
  const [nameInput, setNameInput] = useState(initialName);
  const [avatarInput, setAvatarInput] = useState(initialAvatarUrl);
  const [urlInput, setUrlInput] = useState(
    initialAvatarUrl.startsWith('https://') ? initialAvatarUrl : ''
  );
  const [previewBroken, setPreviewBroken] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const wasOpenRef = useRef(false);
  const nameInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  // Initialize form fields only when the modal transitions from closed -> open
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open && !wasOpenRef.current) {
      setNameInput(initialName);
      setAvatarInput(initialAvatarUrl);
      setUrlInput(initialAvatarUrl.startsWith('https://') ? initialAvatarUrl : '');
      setPreviewBroken(false);
      setErrorMsg(null);
    }
    wasOpenRef.current = open;
  }, [open, initialName, initialAvatarUrl]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const activePreview = avatarInput || fallbackAuthPhoto;
  const previewInitial = (nameInput.trim() || 'I').charAt(0).toUpperCase();

  const handleFileChange = async (file: File) => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const compressed = await compressAvatarFile(file);
      setAvatarInput(compressed);
      setUrlInput('');
      setPreviewBroken(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Gagal memproses gambar.');
    }
  };

  const handleApplyUrl = (nextUrl: string) => {
    setUrlInput(nextUrl);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    const trimmed = nextUrl.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      setAvatarInput('');
      setPreviewBroken(false);
      return;
    }
    if (!trimmed.startsWith('https://')) {
      setErrorMsg('URL foto harus diawali dengan https://');
      return;
    }
    if (!isValidAvatarUrl(trimmed)) {
      setErrorMsg('URL foto terlalu panjang atau tidak valid.');
      return;
    }
    setAvatarInput(trimmed);
    setPreviewBroken(false);
  };

  const handleSelectPreset = (index: number) => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const dataUrl = renderPresetKanjiAvatar(PRESET_AVATARS[index]);
      setAvatarInput(dataUrl);
      setUrlInput('');
      setPreviewBroken(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Gagal memilih avatar.');
    }
  };

  const handleResetDefault = () => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setAvatarInput('');
    setUrlInput('');
    setPreviewBroken(false);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg(null);
    const trimmedName = nameInput.trim();
    if (!trimmedName) {
      setErrorMsg('Nama tampilan tidak boleh kosong.');
      return;
    }
    const trimmedUrl = urlInput.trim();
    if (trimmedUrl) {
      if (!trimmedUrl.startsWith('https://')) {
        setErrorMsg('URL foto harus menggunakan protokol https://');
        return;
      }
      if (!isValidAvatarUrl(trimmedUrl)) {
        setErrorMsg('URL foto tidak valid (pastikan tanpa spasi dan panjangnya wajar).');
        return;
      }
    }
    const finalAvatar = trimmedUrl || avatarInput;
    const ok = await onSave(trimmedName, finalAvatar);
    if (ok) onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      open={open}
      onClose={onClose}
      id="profileModalOverlay"
      ariaLabelledBy="profileModalTitle"
      initialFocusRef={nameInputRef}
      panelClassName="overflow-hidden"
    >
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h2 id="profileModalTitle" className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              Ubah Nama &amp; Foto Profil
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Perubahan tersinkronisasi ke akun Anda.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Tutup editor profil"
            className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-50 inline-flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={e => void handleSubmit(e)} className="p-5 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="relative shrink-0">
              {activePreview && !previewBroken ? (
                <img
                  src={activePreview}
                  alt="Pratinjau foto profil"
                  referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  onError={() => setPreviewBroken(true)}
                  className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-2 border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-800 object-cover bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800"
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-2 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-700 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 font-bold text-xl flex items-center justify-center">
                  {previewInitial}
                </div>
              )}
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-900"
              >
                <Camera className="w-3 h-3" />
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
                className="min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                Unggah Foto
              </button>

              {avatarInput ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleResetDefault}
                  className="min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Bawaan Akun
                </button>
              ) : null}

              <input
                ref={fileRef}
                type="file"
                accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"
                className="sr-only"
                aria-label="Pilih file foto profil"
                onChange={e => {
                  const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                  if (file) void handleFileChange(file);
                  e.target.value = '';
                }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-2">
              Atau Pilih Avatar Kanji
            </p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-6 gap-2">
              {PRESET_AVATARS.map((preset, idx) => (
                <button
                  key={preset.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleSelectPreset(idx)}
                  aria-label={`Pilih avatar ${preset.label}`}
                  title={preset.label}
                  style={{
                    background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${preset.bgFrom}, ${preset.bgTo})`,
                  }}
                  className="aspect-square rounded-full text-white font-jp font-bold text-base flex items-center justify-center hover:scale-105 transition-transform cursor-pointer border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-800 shadow-xs focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
                >
                  {preset.kanji}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <label
                htmlFor="profileNameInput"
                className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200"
              >
                Nama Tampilan
              </label>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-mono-num">
                {nameInput.length}/{DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH}
              </span>
            </div>
            <input
              ref={nameInputRef}
              id="profileNameInput"
              type="text"
              required
              maxLength={DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH}
              value={nameInput}
              onChange={e => setNameInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Nama Anda"
              className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor="profileUrlInput"
              className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-1"
            >
              URL Foto Profil (opsional, HTTPS)
            </label>
            <input
              id="profileUrlInput"
              type="url"
              value={urlInput}
              onChange={e => handleApplyUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://contoh.com/foto.jpg"
              className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
            />
          </div>

          {errorMsg ? (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-200"
            >
              {errorMsg}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer"
            >
              Batal
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {saving ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Profil'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/QuizPanel.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type {
  BookData,
  BunpouPattern,
  ChapterNumber,
  QuizFocus,
  QuizQuestion,
  QuizRecord,
  QuizSessionHistoryEntry,
} from '../types/bunpou';
import {
  countQuizBankSize,
  formatChapterSelectionLabel,
  generateQuizQuestions,
} from '../utils/quizGenerator';
import { buildQuizAnswerSnapshots, type RecordQuizSessionInput } from '../utils/quizStats';
import { QuizAnswerHistoryModal } from './quiz/QuizAnswerHistoryModal';
import type { QuizCountOption } from './quiz/quizHelpers';
import { QuizResultView } from './quiz/QuizResultView';
import { QuizRunView } from './quiz/QuizRunView';
import { QuizSetupView } from './quiz/QuizSetupView';
import { QuizStatsPanel } from './quiz/QuizStatsPanel';

type QuizPhase = 'setup' | 'running' | 'result';

interface QuizPanelProps {
  book: BookData;
  allPatterns: BunpouPattern[];
  showFurigana: boolean;
  showTranslation: boolean;
  history: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[];
  historySyncing: boolean;
  cloudEnabled: boolean;
  onRecordQuizSession: (input: RecordQuizSessionInput) => void;
  onClearQuizHistory: () => void;
}

export function QuizPanel({
  book,
  allPatterns,
  showFurigana,
  showTranslation,
  history,
  historySyncing,
  cloudEnabled,
  onRecordQuizSession,
  onClearQuizHistory,
}: QuizPanelProps) {
  const bookChapterNumbers = useMemo<ChapterNumber[]>(
    () => book.chapters.map(c => c.chapterNumber),
    [book.chapters]
  );

  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<QuizPhase>('setup');
  const [selectedChapters, setSelectedChapters] = useState<ChapterNumber[]>(
    () => book.chapters.map(c => c.chapterNumber)
  );
  const [focus, setFocus] = useState<QuizFocus>('mixed');
  const [questionCount, setQuestionCount] = useState<QuizCountOption>(10);

  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<QuizQuestion[]>([]);
  const [currentIdx, setCurrentIdx] = useState(0);
  const [chosenIndex, setChosenIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<QuizRecord[]>([]);
  const [inspectedHistory, setInspectedHistory] = useState<QuizSessionHistoryEntry | null>(
    null
  );

  // Reset active run & chapter selection if book changes
  useEffect(() => {
    setPhase('setup');
    setSelectedChapters(book.chapters.map(c => c.chapterNumber));
    setQuestions([]);
    setRecords([]);
    setCurrentIdx(0);
    setChosenIndex(null);
    setInspectedHistory(null);
  }, [book]);

  const bankSize = useMemo(
    () =>
      countQuizBankSize(allPatterns, {
        bookId: book.id,
        chapters: selectedChapters,
        focus,
      }),
    [allPatterns, book.id, selectedChapters, focus]
  );

  const scopeLabel = useMemo(
    () => formatChapterSelectionLabel(selectedChapters, bookChapterNumbers.length),
    [selectedChapters, bookChapterNumbers.length]
  );

  const handleToggleChapter = (ch: ChapterNumber) => {
    setSelectedChapters(prev =>
      prev.includes(ch)
        ? prev.filter(item => item !== ch)
        : [...prev, ch].sort((a, b) => a - b)
    );
  };

  const startQuiz = (overrideCount?: QuizCountOption) => {
    const activeCount = overrideCount ?? questionCount;
    const resolvedCount =
      activeCount === 'all' ? 'all' : Math.max(1, Math.min(activeCount, bankSize));
    const generated = generateQuizQuestions(allPatterns, {
      bookId: book.id,
      chapters: selectedChapters,
      focus,
      count: resolvedCount,
      seed: Date.now(),
    });
    if (generated.length === 0) return;
    setQuestions(generated);
    setCurrentIdx(0);
    setChosenIndex(null);
    setRecords([]);
    setPhase('running');
  };

  const handleSelectOption = (optionIndex: number) => {
    if (chosenIndex !== null) return;
    const q = questions[currentIdx];
    if (!q) return;
    setChosenIndex(optionIndex);
    const isCorrect = optionIndex === q.correctIndex;
    const newRecord: QuizRecord = {
      no: currentIdx + 1,
      question: q,
      chosenIndex: optionIndex,
      chosenLabel: q.options[optionIndex] ?? '',
      correctLabel: q.options[q.correctIndex] ?? '',
      isCorrect,
    };
    setRecords(prev => [...prev, newRecord]);
  };

  const handleNextQuestion = () => {
    if (currentIdx + 1 < questions.length) {
      setCurrentIdx(idx => idx + 1);
      setChosenIndex(null);
      return;
    }

    // Finish & persist session
    const total = records.length;
    const correct = records.filter(r => r.isCorrect).length;
    onRecordQuizSession({
      bookId: book.id,
      scopeLabel,
      chapters:
        selectedChapters.length > 0 && selectedChapters.length < bookChapterNumbers.length
          ? selectedChapters
          : undefined,
      focus,
      correct,
      total,
      answers: buildQuizAnswerSnapshots(records),
    });
    setPhase('result');
  };

  const correctSoFar = records.filter(r => r.isCorrect).length;

  return (
    <section aria-label="Kuis interaktif Irodori Bunpou" className="space-y-5">
      {phase === 'setup' ? (
        <>
          <QuizSetupView
            book={book}
            selectedChapters={selectedChapters}
            focus={focus}
            questionCount={questionCount}
            bankSize={bankSize}
            onToggleChapter={handleToggleChapter}
            onSetChapters={setSelectedChapters}
            onSelectFocus={setFocus}
            onSelectCount={setQuestionCount}
            onStartQuiz={startQuiz}
          />

          <QuizStatsPanel
            bookShortTitle={book.shortTitle}
            history={history}
            syncing={historySyncing}
            cloudEnabled={cloudEnabled}
            onInspectEntry={setInspectedHistory}
            onClearHistory={onClearQuizHistory}
          />
        </>
      ) : phase === 'running' && questions[currentIdx] ? (
        <QuizRunView
          question={questions[currentIdx]}
          questionIndex={currentIdx}
          totalQuestions={questions.length}
          correctSoFar={correctSoFar}
          chosenIndex={chosenIndex}
          showFurigana={showFurigana}
          showTranslation={showTranslation}
          onSelectOption={handleSelectOption}
          onNext={handleNextQuestion}
          onCancel={() => setPhase('setup')}
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <QuizResultView
            records={records}
            scopeLabel={scopeLabel}
            focus={focus}
            bookShortTitle={book.shortTitle}
            showFurigana={showFurigana}
            onRetry={() => startQuiz()}
            onBackToSetup={() => setPhase('setup')}
          />

          <QuizStatsPanel
            bookShortTitle={book.shortTitle}
            history={history}
            syncing={historySyncing}
            cloudEnabled={cloudEnabled}
            onInspectEntry={setInspectedHistory}
            onClearHistory={onClearQuizHistory}
          />
        </>
      )}

      <QuizAnswerHistoryModal
        entry={inspectedHistory}
        showFurigana={showFurigana}
        onClose={() => setInspectedHistory(null)}
      />
    </section>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/RestoreModal.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, FolderInput, Info, Merge, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import type { PendingRestoreState } from '../types/bunpou';
import { Modal } from './Modal';

interface RestoreModalProps {
  pending: PendingRestoreState | null;
  currentMasteredCount: number;
  currentHardCount: number;
  mergedMasteredCount: number;
  mergedHardCount: number;
  cleanBackupHardCount: number;
  onConfirm: (mode: 'merge' | 'replace') => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function RestoreModal({
  pending,
  currentMasteredCount,
  currentHardCount,
  mergedMasteredCount,
  mergedHardCount,
  cleanBackupHardCount,
  onConfirm,
  onClose,
}: RestoreModalProps) {
  const mergeBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const [confirmEmptyReplace, setConfirmEmptyReplace] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setConfirmEmptyReplace(false);
  }, [pending]);

  if (!pending) return null;

  const { parsed, fileName } = pending;
  const { impMastered, rejectedIds, exportedAt, formatType, isEmptyBackup } = parsed;
  const hasCurrentProgress = currentMasteredCount > 0 || currentHardCount > 0;

  const dateLabel =
    exportedAt.length >= 10
      ? `Backup ${exportedAt.slice(0, 10)}`
      : formatType !== 'v1'
        ? 'Format backup versi lama'
        : fileName;

  const handleReplaceClick = () => {
    if (isEmptyBackup && hasCurrentProgress && !confirmEmptyReplace) {
      setConfirmEmptyReplace(true);
      return;
    }
    onConfirm('replace');
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      open={Boolean(pending)}
      onClose={onClose}
      id="restoreModal"
      ariaLabelledBy="restoreModalTitle"
      ariaDescribedBy="restoreModalDesc"
      initialFocusRef={mergeBtnRef}
      panelClassName="p-6 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
    >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <FolderInput
            className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shrink-0"
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          <h2
            id="restoreModalTitle"
            className="text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50"
          >
            Pulihkan Data Progres
          </h2>
        </div>
        <p
          id="restoreModalDesc"
          className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed"
        >
          Pilih cara memulihkan data dari file backup agar progres belajar bunpou yang sudah ada
          tidak hilang tanpa sengaja.
        </p>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mt-4">
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-3">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Progres Saat Ini
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 font-mono-num">
              {currentMasteredCount} hafal · {currentHardCount} sulit
            </div>
            <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Tersimpan di browser
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-50/70 dark:bg-indigo-950/50 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800/80 p-3">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">
              File Backup
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 font-mono-num">
              {impMastered.length} hafal · {cleanBackupHardCount} sulit
            </div>
            <div
              className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-indigo-700/80 dark:text-indigo-300/90 truncate"
              title={dateLabel}
            >
              {dateLabel}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isEmptyBackup ? (
          <div
            id="rcEmptyWarn"
            role="alert"
            className={`mt-3 rounded-xl border p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed font-medium flex items-start gap-2 ${
              hasCurrentProgress
                ? 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/50 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 text-rose-900 dark:text-rose-200'
                : 'bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950/50 border-sky-200 dark:border-sky-800/80 text-sky-900 dark:text-sky-200'
            }`}
          >
            {hasCurrentProgress ? (
              <AlertTriangle
                className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400"
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            ) : (
              <Info
                className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400"
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            )}
            <span>
              {hasCurrentProgress
                ? `Peringatan Backup Kosong: File ini berisi 0 pola hafal dan 0 pola sulit. Jika memilih "Timpa Semua", seluruh progres saat ini (${currentMasteredCount} hafal, ${currentHardCount} sulit) akan terhapus menjadi 0.`
                : 'File backup ini kosong (0 pola hafal dan 0 pola sulit). Tidak ada data progres baru yang akan ditambahkan.'}
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {rejectedIds.length > 0 ? (
          <div
            id="rcRejectedWarn"
            role="status"
            className="mt-2.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/80 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/50 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed font-medium text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 flex items-start gap-2"
          >
            <AlertTriangle
              className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <span>
              {rejectedIds.length} ID data tidak cocok dengan buku aktif dan akan diabaikan:{' '}
              {rejectedIds.slice(0, 4).join(', ')}
              {rejectedIds.length > 4 ? ` (+${rejectedIds.length - 4} lainnya)` : ''}.
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {confirmEmptyReplace ? (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-300 dark:border-rose-700 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/60 p-3 text-xs text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-100"
          >
            <p className="font-bold">Konfirmasi Timpa Progres</p>
            <p className="mt-1 leading-relaxed">
              Anda yakin ingin menimpa {currentMasteredCount} hafal &amp; {currentHardCount} sulit
              dengan backup kosong (menjadi 0)? Klik tombol &ldquo;Konfirmasi Hapus &amp; Timpa&rdquo;
              di bawah untuk melanjutkan.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 mt-5">
          <button
            ref={mergeBtnRef}
            type="button"
            id="btnRestoreMerge"
            onClick={() => onConfirm('merge')}
            className="w-full rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm font-bold">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Merge className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                Gabungkan (Merge)
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-emerald-100 font-mono-num whitespace-nowrap">
                → {mergedMasteredCount} hafal · {mergedHardCount} sulit
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-emerald-50/90 leading-snug">
              Simpan progres saat ini dan tambahkan data dari file backup (aman).
            </p>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnRestoreReplace"
            onClick={handleReplaceClick}
            className={`w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 cursor-pointer ${
              confirmEmptyReplace
                ? 'bg-rose-600 border-rose-700 text-white hover:bg-rose-700'
                : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/50 hover:border-rose-300 dark:hover:border-rose-700'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm font-bold">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                {confirmEmptyReplace ? 'Konfirmasi Hapus & Timpa Semua' : 'Timpa Semua (Replace)'}
              </span>
              <span
                className={`text-xs font-semibold font-mono-num whitespace-nowrap ${
                  confirmEmptyReplace ? 'text-rose-100' : 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400'
                }`}
              >
                → {impMastered.length} hafal · {cleanBackupHardCount} sulit
              </span>
            </div>
            <p
              className={`mt-1 text-xs leading-snug ${
                confirmEmptyReplace ? 'text-rose-50' : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'
              }`}
            >
              Hapus progres saat ini dan ganti seluruhnya dengan isi file backup.
            </p>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnRestoreCancel"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-full rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50 transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 cursor-pointer"
          >
            Batal
          </button>
        </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/Sidebar.tsx
================================================
import { BookMarked, CheckCircle2, ClipboardCheck, Layers, List } from 'lucide-react';
import type { AppTab, BookData, ChapterFilter } from '../types/bunpou';

interface SidebarProps {
  activeTab: AppTab;
  activeChapter: ChapterFilter;
  book: BookData;
  masteredSet: Set<string>;
  hardSet: Set<string>;
  onSelectTab: (tab: AppTab) => void;
  onSelectChapter: (chapter: ChapterFilter) => void;
}

const MODE_ITEMS: { id: AppTab; label: string; shortLabel: string; icon: typeof Layers }[] = [
  { id: 'card', label: 'Kartu Hafalan', shortLabel: 'Kartu', icon: Layers },
  { id: 'list', label: 'Daftar Bunpou', shortLabel: 'Daftar', icon: List },
  { id: 'quiz', label: 'Kuis Latihan', shortLabel: 'Kuis', icon: ClipboardCheck },
  { id: 'guide', label: 'Singkatan & Info', shortLabel: 'Singkatan', icon: BookMarked },
];

export function Sidebar({
  activeTab,
  activeChapter,
  book,
  masteredSet,
  hardSet,
  onSelectTab,
  onSelectChapter,
}: SidebarProps) {
  const totalBookPatterns = book.totalPatterns;
  const totalBookMastered = book.chapters.reduce(
    (sum, ch) => sum + ch.patterns.filter(p => masteredSet.has(p.id)).length,
    0
  );
  const totalBookHard = book.chapters.reduce(
    (sum, ch) => sum + ch.patterns.filter(p => hardSet.has(p.id)).length,
    0
  );
  const bookPct =
    totalBookPatterns > 0 ? Math.round((totalBookMastered / totalBookPatterns) * 100) : 0;
  const firstCh = book.chapters[0]?.chapterNumber ?? 1;
  const lastCh = book.chapters[book.chapters.length - 1]?.chapterNumber ?? 18;
  const allChaptersLabel = `Semua Bab (${firstCh}–${lastCh})`;

  return (
    <aside aria-label="Navigasi mode dan bab" className="space-y-4">
      {/* Mode Navigation Card */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-2 shadow-sm">
        <div
          role="tablist"
          aria-label="Mode belajar"
          className="grid grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-1 gap-1.5"
        >
          {MODE_ITEMS.map(item => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            const isActive = activeTab === item.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                id={`tab-${item.id}`}
                aria-selected={isActive}
                onClick={() => onSelectTab(item.id)}
                className={`min-h-[42px] px-2.5 lg:px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-center justify-center lg:justify-start gap-1 lg:gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                    : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-100'
                }`}
              >
                <Icon className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span className="lg:hidden leading-tight">{item.shortLabel}</span>
                <span className="hidden lg:inline">{item.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Chapter Filter — visible for Kartu & Daftar */}
      {activeTab === 'card' || activeTab === 'list' ? (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-3.5 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2.5">
            <h2 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Filter Bab ({book.shortTitle})
            </h2>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono-num font-bold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">
              {bookPct}%
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile select dropdown */}
          <div className="lg:hidden">
            <label htmlFor="mobileChapterSelect" className="sr-only">
              Pilih Bab
            </label>
            <select
              id="mobileChapterSelect"
              value={activeChapter === 'all' ? 'all' : String(activeChapter)}
              onChange={e => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                onSelectChapter(val === 'all' ? 'all' : (Number(val) as ChapterFilter));
              }}
              className="w-full min-h-[42px] rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm font-semibold px-3 py-2 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
            >
              <option value="all">
                {allChaptersLabel} · {totalBookMastered}/{totalBookPatterns} hafal
              </option>
              {book.chapters.map(ch => {
                const chMastered = ch.patterns.filter(p => masteredSet.has(p.id)).length;
                return (
                  <option key={ch.id} value={ch.chapterNumber}>
                    Bab {ch.chapterNumber}: {ch.titleJp} — {ch.titleId} ({chMastered}/
                    {ch.patterns.length})
                  </option>
                );
              })}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Desktop vertical chapter list */}
          <div className="hidden lg:block space-y-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnChapter-all"
              onClick={() => onSelectChapter('all')}
              className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                activeChapter === 'all'
                  ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-200 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800'
                  : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/80 border border-transparent'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{allChaptersLabel}</span>
              <span className="font-mono-num text-[11px] font-bold">
                {totalBookMastered}/{totalBookPatterns}
              </span>
            </button>

            <div className="max-h-[460px] overflow-y-auto pr-1 space-y-1 pt-1">
              {book.chapters.map(ch => {
                const isSelected = activeChapter === ch.chapterNumber;
                const chMastered = ch.patterns.filter(p => masteredSet.has(p.id)).length;
                const chTotal = ch.patterns.length;
                const isComplete = chTotal > 0 && chMastered === chTotal;

                return (
                  <button
                    key={ch.id}
                    type="button"
                    id={`btnChapter-${ch.chapterNumber}`}
                    onClick={() => onSelectChapter(ch.chapterNumber)}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 border ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-900 dark:text-indigo-100 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800'
                        : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/80 border-transparent'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0">
                        <span className="shrink-0">Bab {ch.chapterNumber}</span>
                        {isComplete ? (
                          <CheckCircle2
                            className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0"
                            aria-label="Bab selesai"
                          />
                        ) : null}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-mono-num font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shrink-0">
                        {chMastered}/{chTotal}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="font-jp text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 truncate mt-0.5">
                      {ch.titleJp}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">
                      {ch.titleId}
                    </p>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-3 pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            <span>
              Hafal:{' '}
              <strong className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-mono-num">
                {totalBookMastered}
              </strong>
            </span>
            <span>
              Sulit:{' '}
              <strong className="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 font-mono-num">
                {totalBookHard}
              </strong>
            </span>
            <span>
              Total:{' '}
              <strong className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-mono-num">
                {totalBookPatterns}
              </strong>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </aside>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ToastBanner.tsx
================================================
import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, Info, X, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ToastMessage } from '../types/bunpou';

interface ToastBannerProps {
  toast: ToastMessage | null;
  onDismiss: () => void;
}

export function ToastBanner({ toast, onDismiss }: ToastBannerProps) {
  if (!toast) return null;

  const toneStyles: Record<ToastMessage['tone'], string> = {
    success:
      'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/95 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100',
    error:
      'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/95 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800 text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-100',
    warning:
      'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/95 border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 text-amber-950 dark:text-amber-100',
    info: 'bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950/95 border-sky-200 dark:border-sky-800 text-sky-950 dark:text-sky-100',
  };

  const Icon =
    toast.tone === 'success'
      ? CheckCircle2
      : toast.tone === 'error'
        ? XCircle
        : toast.tone === 'warning'
          ? AlertTriangle
          : Info;

  return (
    <div
      role={toast.tone === 'error' || toast.tone === 'warning' ? 'alert' : 'status'}
      aria-live="polite"
      className="fixed top-16 sm:top-auto sm:bottom-4 right-4 left-4 sm:left-auto sm:max-w-md z-50"
    >
      <div
        className={`flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border p-4 shadow-lg ${toneStyles[toast.tone]}`}
      >
        <Icon className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0 mt-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-sm">
          <p className="font-semibold leading-snug">{toast.title}</p>
          {toast.detail ? (
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs opacity-90 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
              {toast.detail}
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onDismiss}
          aria-label="Tutup notifikasi"
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/quiz/QuizAnswerHistoryModal.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, X, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { QuizSessionHistoryEntry } from '../../types/bunpou';
import { AudioButton } from '../AudioButton';
import { JapaneseText } from '../JapaneseText';
import { Modal } from '../Modal';
import {
  formatShortDate,
  getFocusLabel,
  getQuestionTypeLabel,
  getScoreToneClass,
} from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizAnswerHistoryModalProps {
  entry: QuizSessionHistoryEntry | null;
  showFurigana: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function QuizAnswerHistoryModal({
  entry,
  showFurigana,
  onClose,
}: QuizAnswerHistoryModalProps) {
  const [onlyWrong, setOnlyWrong] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setOnlyWrong(false);
  }, [entry?.id]);

  if (!entry) return null;

  const answers = entry.answers ?? [];
  const wrongCount = answers.filter(a => !a.correct).length;
  const displayed = onlyWrong ? answers.filter(a => !a.correct) : answers;

  return (
    <Modal
      open={Boolean(entry)}
      onClose={onClose}
      id="quizHistoryModal"
      ariaLabelledBy="quizHistoryModalTitle"
      maxWidthClass="max-w-2xl"
      panelClassName="max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden"
    >
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h3
              id="quizHistoryModalTitle"
              className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50"
            >
              Detail Riwayat Kuis ·{' '}
              <span className={`font-mono-num ${getScoreToneClass(entry.score)}`}>
                {entry.score}%
              </span>
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              {entry.scopeLabel} · {getFocusLabel(entry.focus)} · Benar {entry.correct}/{entry.total}{' '}
              · {formatShortDate(entry.completedAt)}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Tutup detail riwayat"
            className="min-h-[36px] min-w-[36px] p-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 inline-flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {answers.length > 0 && wrongCount > 0 ? (
          <div className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOnlyWrong(false)}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer ${
                !onlyWrong
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'
              }`}
            >
              Semua ({answers.length})
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOnlyWrong(true)}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer ${
                onlyWrong
                  ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'
              }`}
            >
              Salah ({wrongCount})
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <div className="p-5 overflow-y-auto space-y-2.5 flex-1">
          {displayed.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-center py-6">
              Detail per soal tidak tersimpan untuk sesi ini.
            </p>
          ) : (
            displayed.map(ans => (
              <div
                key={`${ans.no}-${ans.patternId}`}
                className={`rounded-xl border p-3.5 space-y-1.5 ${
                  ans.correct
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50/40 dark:bg-emerald-950/20 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/70'
                    : 'bg-rose-50/50 dark:bg-rose-950/25 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/70'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                    {ans.correct ? (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                    ) : (
                      <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                    )}
                    <span className="text-xs font-mono-num font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      #{ans.no}
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-jp text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 truncate">
                      {ans.patternTitle}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shrink-0">
                    {getQuestionTypeLabel(ans.type)}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {ans.formula ? (
                  <p className="text-xs font-jp text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 font-semibold">
                    Rumus: {ans.formula}
                  </p>
                ) : null}

                {ans.exampleJp ? (
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-0.5">
                    <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                      <JapaneseText text={ans.exampleJp} showFurigana={showFurigana} />
                    </p>
                    <AudioButton text={ans.exampleJp} />
                  </div>
                ) : null}

                {!ans.correct ? (
                  <p className="text-xs text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300">
                    Jawaban dipilih: <strong className="font-jp">{ans.chosenLabel}</strong> · Benar:{' '}
                    <strong className="font-jp text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300">
                      {ans.expectedLabel}
                    </strong>
                  </p>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}
