FILE: src/components/quiz/quizHelpers.ts
================================================
import type { QuizFocus, QuizQuestionType } from '../../types/bunpou';

export type QuizCountOption = number | 'all';

export const QUIZ_COUNT_PRESETS = [5, 10, 20] as const;

export const QUIZ_COUNT_OPTIONS: readonly { value: QuizCountOption; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 5, label: '5 Soal' },
  { value: 10, label: '10 Soal' },
  { value: 20, label: '20 Soal' },
  { value: 'all', label: 'Semua' },
] as const;

export const QUIZ_FOCUS_OPTIONS: readonly {
  id: QuizFocus;
  label: string;
  desc: string;
}[] = [
  {
    id: 'mixed',
    label: 'Campuran (JFT/JLPT)',
    desc: 'Gabungan rumpang partikel, rumus penyambungan, dan fungsi.',
  },
  {
    id: 'cloze',
    label: 'Isian Rumpang',
    desc: 'Lengkapi partikel atau akhiran pada contoh kalimat asli.',
  },
  {
    id: 'formula',
    label: 'Rumus Penyambungan',
    desc: 'Cocokkan pola bunpou dengan rumus konjugasi yang benar.',
  },
  {
    id: 'function',
    label: 'Fungsi & Makna',
    desc: 'Identifikasi kegunaan utama dari pola tata bahasa Jepang.',
  },
] as const;

export function getFocusLabel(focus: QuizFocus): string {
  switch (focus) {
    case 'cloze':
      return 'Isian Rumpang';
    case 'formula':
      return 'Rumus';
    case 'function':
      return 'Fungsi';
    default:
      return 'Campuran';
  }
}

export function getQuestionTypeLabel(type: QuizQuestionType): string {
  switch (type) {
    case 'cloze':
      return 'Isian Rumpang';
    case 'formula':
      return 'Rumus Penyambungan';
    case 'function':
      return 'Fungsi Bunpou';
  }
}

export function getScoreToneClass(score: number): string {
  if (score >= 80) return 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400';
  if (score >= 60) return 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400';
  return 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400';
}

export function getScoreBadgeClass(score: number): string {
  if (score >= 80) {
    return 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-200';
  }
  if (score >= 60) {
    return 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/60 border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200';
  }
  return 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/60 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800 text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-200';
}

export function getScoreMessage(score: number): string {
  if (score >= 90) return 'Luar biasa! Pemahaman tata bahasa Anda sangat kuat.';
  if (score >= 75) return 'Bagus sekali! Sedikit lagi menuju penguasaan penuh.';
  if (score >= 55) return 'Cukup baik. Tinjau kembali pola yang masih keliru di bawah.';
  return 'Tetap semangat! Pelajari ulang rumus dan contoh di kartu hafalan.';
}

export function formatShortDate(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return 'Baru saja';
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
      day: '2-digit',
      month: 'short',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    }).format(date);
  } catch {
    return date.toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' ');
  }
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Award, CheckCircle2, RotateCcw, Settings2, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { QuizFocus, QuizRecord } from '../../types/bunpou';
import { calculateQuizScore } from '../../utils/quizGenerator';
import { AudioButton } from '../AudioButton';
import { FormulaDisplay } from '../FormulaDisplay';
import { JapaneseText } from '../JapaneseText';
import {
  getFocusLabel,
  getQuestionTypeLabel,
  getScoreBadgeClass,
  getScoreMessage,
  getScoreToneClass,
} from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizResultViewProps {
  records: QuizRecord[];
  scopeLabel: string;
  focus: QuizFocus;
  bookShortTitle: string;
  showFurigana: boolean;
  onRetry: () => void;
  onBackToSetup: () => void;
}

export function QuizResultView({
  records,
  scopeLabel,
  focus,
  bookShortTitle,
  showFurigana,
  onRetry,
  onBackToSetup,
}: QuizResultViewProps) {
  const [onlyWrong, setOnlyWrong] = useState(false);

  const total = records.length;
  const correct = records.filter(r => r.isCorrect).length;
  const wrongCount = total - correct;
  const score = calculateQuizScore(correct, total);

  const displayed = onlyWrong ? records.filter(r => !r.isCorrect) : records;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Score Summary Card */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm text-center space-y-4">
        <div
          className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl border inline-flex items-center justify-center mx-auto ${getScoreBadgeClass(score)}`}
        >
          <Award className="w-7 h-7" aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            {bookShortTitle} · {scopeLabel} · {getFocusLabel(focus)}
          </p>
          <p className={`text-4xl sm:text-5xl font-extrabold font-mono-num ${getScoreToneClass(score)}`}>
            {score}%
          </p>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
            Benar <span className="font-mono-num">{correct}</span> dari{' '}
            <span className="font-mono-num">{total}</span> soal
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto pt-1">
            {getScoreMessage(score)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2.5 pt-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnQuizRetry"
            onClick={onRetry}
            className="min-h-[44px] px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>Ulangi Kuis</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnQuizBackSetup"
            onClick={onBackToSetup}
            className="min-h-[44px] px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Settings2 className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>Atur Kuis Baru</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Review List */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
            Pembahasan Jawaban ({displayed.length})
          </h3>
          {wrongCount > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setOnlyWrong(false)}
                aria-pressed={!onlyWrong}
                className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                  !onlyWrong
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'
                }`}
              >
                Semua ({total})
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setOnlyWrong(true)}
                aria-pressed={onlyWrong}
                className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                  onlyWrong
                    ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'
                }`}
              >
                Salah ({wrongCount})
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {displayed.map(rec => (
            <div
              key={rec.no}
              className={`rounded-xl border p-4 space-y-2.5 ${
                rec.isCorrect
                  ? 'bg-emerald-50/40 dark:bg-emerald-950/20 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/70'
                  : 'bg-rose-50/50 dark:bg-rose-950/25 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/70'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                  {rec.isCorrect ? (
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                  ) : (
                    <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                  )}
                  <span className="text-xs font-mono-num font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    #{rec.no}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-jp text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 truncate">
                    {rec.question.pattern.title}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shrink-0">
                  Bab {rec.question.pattern.chapterNumber} · {getQuestionTypeLabel(rec.question.type)}
                </span>
              </div>

              {rec.question.japaneseFull ? (
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-200/70 dark:border-slate-800 p-2.5">
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    <JapaneseText text={rec.question.japaneseFull} showFurigana={showFurigana} />
                  </p>
                  <AudioButton text={rec.question.japaneseFull} />
                </div>
              ) : null}

              <div className="text-xs space-y-1">
                {!rec.isCorrect ? (
                  <p className="text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300">
                    Jawaban Anda: <strong className="font-jp">{rec.chosenLabel}</strong>
                  </p>
                ) : null}
                <p className="text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300">
                  Jawaban Benar: <strong className="font-jp">{rec.correctLabel}</strong>
                </p>
              </div>

              <FormulaDisplay formulas={rec.question.pattern.formulas} compact />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { ArrowRight, CheckCircle2, X, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { QuizQuestion } from '../../types/bunpou';
import { ABC_LABELS } from '../../utils/quizGenerator';
import { AudioButton } from '../AudioButton';
import { FormulaDisplay } from '../FormulaDisplay';
import { JapaneseText } from '../JapaneseText';
import { getQuestionTypeLabel } from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizRunViewProps {
  question: QuizQuestion;
  questionIndex: number;
  totalQuestions: number;
  correctSoFar: number;
  chosenIndex: number | null;
  showFurigana: boolean;
  showTranslation: boolean;
  onSelectOption: (optionIndex: number) => void;
  onNext: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export function QuizRunView({
  question,
  questionIndex,
  totalQuestions,
  correctSoFar,
  chosenIndex,
  showFurigana,
  showTranslation,
  onSelectOption,
  onNext,
  onCancel,
}: QuizRunViewProps) {
  const answered = chosenIndex !== null;
  const isCorrect = answered && chosenIndex === question.correctIndex;
  const isLast = questionIndex + 1 >= totalQuestions;
  const progressPct = Math.round(((questionIndex + (answered ? 1 : 0)) / totalQuestions) * 100);

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-sm space-y-5">
      {/* Header bar */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-200 text-xs font-bold">
              {getQuestionTypeLabel(question.type)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Bab {question.pattern.chapterNumber} · {question.pattern.bookShortTitle}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-mono-num font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
              Soal {questionIndex + 1}/{totalQuestions}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono-num font-semibold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg">
              Benar: {correctSoFar}
            </span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onCancel}
              aria-label="Keluar dari kuis"
              title="Batalkan sesi kuis"
              className="min-h-[34px] min-w-[34px] p-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-100 inline-flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 overflow-hidden">
          <div
            className="h-full rounded-full bg-indigo-600 dark:bg-indigo-500 transition-all duration-300"
            style={{ width: `${progressPct}%` }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Question Prompt */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-slate-700/70 p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3">
        <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 leading-relaxed">
          {showTranslation ? question.prompt : (question.promptWithoutTranslation ?? question.prompt)}
        </p>

        {question.japanesePrompt ? (
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-3.5 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
            <p className="text-base sm:text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              <JapaneseText
                text={answered && question.japaneseFull ? question.japaneseFull : question.japanesePrompt}
                showFurigana={showFurigana}
              />
            </p>
            {answered && question.japaneseFull ? (
              <AudioButton text={question.japaneseFull} label="Dengar" />
            ) : question.type !== 'cloze' && question.japaneseFull ? (
              <AudioButton text={question.japaneseFull} label="Dengar" />
            ) : null}
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      {/* 4 Options */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5" role="group" aria-label="Pilihan jawaban">
        {question.options.map((opt, idx) => {
          const isSelected = chosenIndex === idx;
          const isRightOption = idx === question.correctIndex;

          let btnStyle =
            'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 hover:border-indigo-400 dark:hover:border-indigo-500 hover:bg-indigo-50/40 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/30';

          if (answered) {
            if (isRightOption) {
              btnStyle =
                'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 border-emerald-500 dark:border-emerald-500 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100';
            } else if (isSelected) {
              btnStyle =
                'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/60 border-rose-500 dark:border-rose-500 text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-100';
            } else {
              btnStyle =
                'bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-800/30 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 opacity-75';
            }
          }

          return (
            <button
              key={idx}
              type="button"
              id={`quizOption-${idx}`}
              disabled={answered}
              onClick={() => onSelectOption(idx)}
              className={`w-full min-h-[52px] text-left px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border-2 transition-all flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-default focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${btnStyle}`}
            >
              <span
                className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono-num font-bold inline-flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                  answered && isRightOption
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                    : answered && isSelected
                      ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white'
                      : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'
                }`}
              >
                {ABC_LABELS[idx]}
              </span>
              <span
                className={`flex-1 text-sm sm:text-base font-semibold leading-snug ${
                  question.type !== 'function' ? 'font-jp' : ''
                }`}
              >
                {opt}
              </span>
              {answered && isRightOption ? (
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              ) : answered && isSelected ? (
                <XCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              ) : null}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Feedback & Next */}
      {answered ? (
        <div
          className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 space-y-3 ${
            isCorrect
              ? 'bg-emerald-50/80 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/80'
              : 'bg-rose-50/80 dark:bg-rose-950/40 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80'
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
            {isCorrect ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <XCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
            <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1.5 flex-1">
              <p
                className={`text-sm font-bold ${
                  isCorrect
                    ? 'text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200'
                    : 'text-rose-900 dark:text-rose-200'
                }`}
              >
                {isCorrect ? 'Jawaban Benar!' : 'Kurang Tepat'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">
                {question.explanation}
              </p>
              <div className="pt-1">
                <FormulaDisplay formulas={question.pattern.formulas} compact />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-end pt-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnQuizNext"
              onClick={onNext}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <span>{isLast ? 'Lihat Hasil Kuis' : 'Soal Berikutnya'}</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/quiz/QuizSetupView.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Check, Play } from 'lucide-react';
import type { BookData, ChapterNumber, QuizFocus } from '../../types/bunpou';
import { formatChapterSelectionLabel } from '../../utils/quizGenerator';
import {
  QUIZ_COUNT_OPTIONS,
  QUIZ_COUNT_PRESETS,
  QUIZ_FOCUS_OPTIONS,
  type QuizCountOption,
} from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizSetupViewProps {
  book: BookData;
  selectedChapters: ChapterNumber[];
  focus: QuizFocus;
  questionCount: QuizCountOption;
  bankSize: number;
  onToggleChapter: (ch: ChapterNumber) => void;
  onSetChapters: (chapters: ChapterNumber[]) => void;
  onSelectFocus: (focus: QuizFocus) => void;
  onSelectCount: (count: QuizCountOption) => void;
  onStartQuiz: (overrideCount?: QuizCountOption) => void;
}

export function QuizSetupView({
  book,
  selectedChapters,
  focus,
  questionCount,
  bankSize,
  onToggleChapter,
  onSetChapters,
  onSelectFocus,
  onSelectCount,
  onStartQuiz,
}: QuizSetupViewProps) {
  const [manualDraft, setManualDraft] = useState<string>(() =>
    questionCount === 'all' ? '' : String(questionCount)
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (questionCount === 'all') {
      setManualDraft('');
    } else {
      setManualDraft(String(questionCount));
    }
  }, [questionCount]);

  const allBookChapters = book.chapters.map(c => c.chapterNumber);
  const firstCh = allBookChapters[0] ?? 1;
  const lastCh = allBookChapters[allBookChapters.length - 1] ?? 18;
  const selectedSet = new Set(selectedChapters);
  const scopeLabel = formatChapterSelectionLabel(selectedChapters, allBookChapters.length);

  const isCustomCount =
    questionCount !== 'all' &&
    !QUIZ_COUNT_PRESETS.includes(questionCount as (typeof QUIZ_COUNT_PRESETS)[number]);

  const effectiveCount =
    bankSize <= 0
      ? 0
      : questionCount === 'all'
        ? bankSize
        : Math.max(1, Math.min(questionCount, bankSize));

  const exceedsBank =
    questionCount !== 'all' && bankSize > 0 && questionCount > bankSize;

  const handleManualInputChange = (raw: string) => {
    const digitsOnly = raw.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 3);
    setManualDraft(digitsOnly);
    if (!digitsOnly) return;
    const parsed = Number.parseInt(digitsOnly, 10);
    if (Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 1) {
      onSelectCount(parsed);
    }
  };

  const commitManualInput = (): QuizCountOption => {
    if (!manualDraft) {
      if (questionCount !== 'all') {
        setManualDraft(String(questionCount));
      }
      return questionCount;
    }
    const parsed = Number.parseInt(manualDraft, 10);
    if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 1) {
      onSelectCount(1);
      setManualDraft('1');
      return 1;
    }
    const clamped = bankSize > 0 ? Math.min(parsed, bankSize) : parsed;
    onSelectCount(clamped);
    setManualDraft(String(clamped));
    return clamped;
  };

  const presets: { label: string; chapters: ChapterNumber[] }[] =
    firstCh === 3
      ? [
          { label: `Semua (${firstCh}–${lastCh})`, chapters: allBookChapters },
          { label: 'Bab 3–7', chapters: [3, 4, 5, 6, 7] },
          { label: 'Bab 8–12', chapters: [8, 9, 10, 11, 12] },
          { label: 'Bab 13–18', chapters: [13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18] },
        ]
      : [
          { label: `Semua (${firstCh}–${lastCh})`, chapters: allBookChapters },
          { label: 'Bab 1–6', chapters: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6] },
          { label: 'Bab 7–12', chapters: [7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12] },
          { label: 'Bab 13–18', chapters: [13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18] },
        ];

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-sm space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
          Pengaturan Kuis · {book.shortTitle}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">
          Pilih bab, jenis soal, dan jumlah pertanyaan untuk latihan gaya JFT-Basic / JLPT.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Multi-Chapter Selector */}
      <div className="space-y-2.5">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
            Cakupan Bab ({scopeLabel})
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
            {presets.map(preset => (
              <button
                key={preset.label}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSetChapters(preset.chapters)}
                className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-indigo-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/60 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                {preset.label}
              </button>
            ))}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSetChapters([])}
              className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/50 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-700 dark:hover:text-rose-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Kosongkan
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-2">
          {book.chapters.map(ch => {
            const num = ch.chapterNumber;
            const active = selectedSet.has(num);
            return (
              <button
                key={ch.id}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={active}
                onClick={() => onToggleChapter(num)}
                title={`Bab ${ch.chapterNumber}: ${ch.titleId} (${ch.patterns.length} pola)`}
                className={`min-h-[42px] px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 border-indigo-400 dark:border-indigo-600 text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-100'
                    : 'bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                <span>Bab {num}</span>
                {active ? (
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-num opacity-70">{ch.patterns.length}</span>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Focus Selector */}
      <div className="space-y-2.5">
        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
          Jenis Latihan
        </p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
          {QUIZ_FOCUS_OPTIONS.map(opt => {
            const active = focus === opt.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.id}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={active}
                onClick={() => onSelectFocus(opt.id)}
                className={`text-left rounded-xl border p-3 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 border-indigo-400 dark:border-indigo-600'
                    : 'bg-slate-50/60 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                <p
                  className={`text-sm font-bold ${
                    active
                      ? 'text-indigo-900 dark:text-indigo-100'
                      : 'text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100'
                  }`}
                >
                  {opt.label}
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                  {opt.desc}
                </p>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Question Count Selector + Dedicated Manual Input + Start CTA */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end justify-between gap-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
              Jumlah Soal
            </p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
              {QUIZ_COUNT_OPTIONS.map(opt => {
                const active = questionCount === opt.value;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={String(opt.value)}
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={active}
                    onClick={() => onSelectCount(opt.value)}
                    className={`min-h-[38px] px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                      active
                        ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 text-white'
                        : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {opt.label}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <label
              htmlFor="inputManualQuizCount"
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 whitespace-nowrap"
            >
              Atau ketik jumlah manual:
            </label>
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Kurangi jumlah soal"
                disabled={bankSize <= 0 || effectiveCount <= 1}
                onClick={() => {
                  const base = questionCount === 'all' ? bankSize : effectiveCount;
                  onSelectCount(Math.max(1, base - 1));
                }}
                className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-bold text-base inline-flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                −
              </button>
              <input
                id="inputManualQuizCount"
                type="text"
                inputMode="numeric"
                pattern="[0-9]*"
                autoComplete="off"
                value={manualDraft}
                placeholder={bankSize > 0 ? `1–${bankSize}` : '0'}
                aria-label="Jumlah soal manual"
                onFocus={e => e.currentTarget.select()}
                onChange={e => handleManualInputChange(e.target.value)}
                onBlur={() => {
                  commitManualInput();
                }}
                onKeyDown={e => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' && bankSize > 0) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const resolved = commitManualInput();
                    onStartQuiz(resolved);
                  }
                }}
                className={`w-24 h-9 px-3 rounded-xl border-2 text-sm font-mono-num font-bold text-center shadow-inner transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/25 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 ${
                  isCustomCount
                    ? 'bg-indigo-50/70 dark:bg-indigo-950/60 border-indigo-500 text-indigo-900 dark:text-indigo-100'
                    : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-slate-900'
                }`}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Tambah jumlah soal"
                disabled={bankSize <= 0 || effectiveCount >= bankSize}
                onClick={() => {
                  const base = questionCount === 'all' ? bankSize : effectiveCount;
                  onSelectCount(Math.min(bankSize, base + 1));
                }}
                className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-bold text-base inline-flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                +
              </button>
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                soal (maks. {bankSize})
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            Bank soal tersedia:{' '}
            <strong className="font-mono-num text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
              {bankSize}
            </strong>{' '}
            soal{' '}
            {exceedsBank
              ? `(dibatasi ke ${bankSize} soal sesuai bank tersedia)`
              : effectiveCount > 0
                ? `(akan dimulai ${effectiveCount} soal)`
                : ''}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          id="btnStartQuiz"
          disabled={effectiveCount <= 0}
          onClick={() => onStartQuiz()}
          className="min-h-[48px] px-6 py-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold text-sm inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors shadow-sm cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-45 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shrink-0"
        >
          <Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" aria-hidden="true" />
          <span>Mulai Kuis ({effectiveCount} Soal)</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { BarChart3, ChevronRight, Cloud, Trash2, TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-react';
import type { QuizSessionHistoryEntry } from '../../types/bunpou';
import { computeQuizPerformanceStats } from '../../utils/quizStats';
import { formatShortDate, getFocusLabel, getScoreToneClass } from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizStatsPanelProps {
  bookShortTitle: string;
  history: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[];
  syncing: boolean;
  cloudEnabled: boolean;
  onInspectEntry: (entry: QuizSessionHistoryEntry) => void;
  onClearHistory: () => void;
}

export function QuizStatsPanel({
  bookShortTitle,
  history,
  syncing,
  cloudEnabled,
  onInspectEntry,
  onClearHistory,
}: QuizStatsPanelProps) {
  const [confirmClear, setConfirmClear] = useState(false);
  const stats = computeQuizPerformanceStats(history);

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <BarChart3 className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" aria-hidden="true" />
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
            Statistik &amp; Riwayat Kuis ({bookShortTitle})
          </h3>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {cloudEnabled ? (
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 inline-flex items-center gap-1">
              <Cloud className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              {syncing ? 'Menyinkronkan...' : 'Tersinkron Cloud'}
            </span>
          ) : null}

          {history.length > 0 ? (
            confirmClear ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onClearHistory();
                    setConfirmClear(false);
                  }}
                  className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Ya, Hapus
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setConfirmClear(false)}
                  className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Batal
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setConfirmClear(true)}
                className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-700 dark:hover:text-rose-300 hover:border-rose-200 dark:hover:border-rose-800 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Hapus Riwayat</span>
              </button>
            )
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      {history.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 py-3">
          Belum ada riwayat kuis untuk {bookShortTitle}. Selesaikan satu sesi kuis untuk melihat
          grafik akurasi dan pembahasan riwayat.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/70 dark:border-slate-700/60 p-3">
              <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                Total Sesi
              </p>
              <p className="text-xl font-extrabold font-mono-num text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 mt-0.5">
                {stats.totalSessions}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/70 dark:border-slate-700/60 p-3">
              <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                Akurasi Total
              </p>
              <p
                className={`text-xl font-extrabold font-mono-num mt-0.5 ${getScoreToneClass(
                  stats.overallAccuracyPct
                )}`}
              >
                {stats.overallAccuracyPct}%
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/70 dark:border-slate-700/60 p-3">
              <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                Skor Tertinggi
              </p>
              <p className="text-xl font-extrabold font-mono-num text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">
                {stats.bestScore}%
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200/70 dark:border-slate-700/60 p-3">
              <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                Skor Terakhir
              </p>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
                <p
                  className={`text-xl font-extrabold font-mono-num ${getScoreToneClass(
                    stats.latestScore ?? 0
                  )}`}
                >
                  {stats.latestScore ?? 0}%
                </p>
                {stats.trendDelta !== null && stats.trendDelta !== 0 ? (
                  <span
                    className={`text-[11px] font-mono-num font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 ${
                      stats.trendDelta > 0
                        ? 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400'
                        : 'text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {stats.trendDelta > 0 ? (
                      <TrendingUp className="w-3 h-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                    ) : (
                      <TrendingDown className="w-3 h-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                    )}
                    {stats.trendDelta > 0 ? `+${stats.trendDelta}` : stats.trendDelta}
                  </span>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2">
            {history.map(entry => (
              <button
                key={entry.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onInspectEntry(entry)}
                className="w-full text-left rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/60 dark:bg-slate-800/40 hover:bg-indigo-50/50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/30 hover:border-indigo-200 dark:hover:border-indigo-800 px-3.5 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 truncate">
                    {entry.scopeLabel} · {getFocusLabel(entry.focus)}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    {formatShortDate(entry.completedAt)} · Benar {entry.correct}/{entry.total}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                  <span
                    className={`text-sm font-mono-num font-extrabold ${getScoreToneClass(
                      entry.score
                    )}`}
                  >
                    {entry.score}%
                  </span>
                  <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                </div>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { AbbreviationItem, ParsedIrodoriDataset } from '../types/bunpou';
import { IRODORI_DASAR1_SPEC } from './irodoriDasar1';
import { IRODORI_DASAR2_SPEC } from './irodoriDasar2';
import { IRODORI_PEMULA_SPEC } from './irodoriPemula';
import { buildBookFromRaw } from './irodoriTypes';

export const IRODORI_ABBREVIATIONS: AbbreviationItem[] = [
  { code: 'N', meaning: 'Kata Benda (名詞 / Meishi) — mis. 日本、会社、うどん、部屋' },
  { code: 'V-ます', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk sopan Masu (マス形) — mis. 食べます、行きます' },
  { code: 'V-る', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk kamus (辞書形) — mis. 食べる、行く、する' },
  { code: 'V-ない', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk negatif biasa (ナイ形) — mis. 食べない、行かない' },
  { code: 'V-て', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk sambung Te (テ形) — mis. 食べて、行って、飲んで' },
  { code: 'V-た', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk lampau biasa (タ形) — mis. 食べた、行った' },
  { code: 'V-（られ）ます', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk Potensial / "bisa" (可能形) — mis. 行けます、食べられます' },
  { code: 'V-（ら）れます', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk Pasif / "di-..." (受身形) — mis. 開催されます、注意されます' },
  { code: 'V-ば', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk Syarat Ba (条件形) — mis. 行けば、着れば、すれば' },
  { code: 'V-（よ）う', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk Ajakan / Niat Volisional (意志形) — mis. 作ろう、食べよう' },
  { code: 'V（命令形）', meaning: 'Kata Kerja bentuk Perintah tegas (命令形) — mis. 急げ、逃げろ、走るな' },
  { code: 'イA', meaning: 'Kata Sifat-i (イ形容詞) — berakhiran い, mis. 暑い、楽しい、おいしい' },
  { code: 'ナA', meaning: 'Kata Sifat-na (ナ形容詞) — memakai な sebelum N, mis. 静か、便利、好き' },
  { code: 'S（普通形）', meaning: 'Kalimat bentuk biasa / kasual (普通体) — dipakai sebelum と思います、そうです、んです' },
  { code: 'S（丁寧体）', meaning: 'Kalimat bentuk sopan (です／ます) — dipakai dalam percakapan formal/sopan' },
  { code: '【…】', meaning: 'Kategori kosakata spesifik — mis. 【場所】 tempat, 【人】 orang, 【時間】 waktu, 【疑問詞】 kata tanya' },
];

const pemulaBook = buildBookFromRaw(IRODORI_PEMULA_SPEC);
const dasar1Book = buildBookFromRaw(IRODORI_DASAR1_SPEC);
const dasar2Book = buildBookFromRaw(IRODORI_DASAR2_SPEC);

const books = [pemulaBook, dasar1Book, dasar2Book];
const allPatterns = books.flatMap(book => book.chapters.flatMap(ch => ch.patterns));

export const IRODORI_DATASET: ParsedIrodoriDataset = {
  abbreviations: IRODORI_ABBREVIATIONS,
  books,
  allPatterns,
};

FILE: src/data/irodoriTypes.ts
================================================
import type { BookData, BookId, BunpouPattern, ChapterData, ChapterNumber } from '../types/bunpou';
import { stripFuriganaMarkup } from '../utils/furiganaConverter';

export interface RawExample {
  /** Japanese sentence with optional inline {漢字|かな} ruby markup */
  jp: string;
  /** Natural Indonesian translation */
  id: string;
}

export interface RawPattern {
  code: string;
  title: string;
  formulas: string[];
  functionText: string;
  marugoto?: string;
  notes?: string[];
  examples: RawExample[];
}

export interface RawChapter {
  chapterNumber: ChapterNumber;
  topicJp: string;
  topicId: string;
  titleJp: string;
  titleId: string;
  patterns: RawPattern[];
}

export interface RawBookSpec {
  id: BookId;
  shortTitle: string;
  title: string;
  japaneseSubtitle: string;
  level: 'A1' | 'A2';
  chapters: RawChapter[];
}

export function buildBookFromRaw(spec: RawBookSpec): BookData {
  const chapters: ChapterData[] = spec.chapters.map(ch => {
    const patterns: BunpouPattern[] = ch.patterns.map((pat, idx) => {
      const patternIndex = idx + 1;
      const notes: string[] = [...(pat.notes ?? [])];
      if (pat.marugoto) {
        notes.push(`Padanan 『まるごと』: ${pat.marugoto}`);
      }
      return {
        id: `${spec.id}-bab-${ch.chapterNumber}-p${patternIndex}`,
        bookId: spec.id,
        bookTitle: spec.title,
        bookShortTitle: spec.shortTitle,
        level: spec.level,
        topicJp: ch.topicJp,
        topicId: ch.topicId,
        chapterNumber: ch.chapterNumber,
        chapterTitleJp: ch.titleJp,
        chapterTitleId: ch.titleId,
        patternIndex,
        itemCode: pat.code,
        title: pat.title,
        shortPattern: pat.title.replace(/\s*＜[^＞]+＞/g, '').trim(),
        formulas: pat.formulas.map(stripFuriganaMarkup),
        functionText: pat.functionText,
        marugotoRef: pat.marugoto,
        notes,
        examples: pat.examples.map(ex => ({
          japanese: ex.jp,
          translation: ex.id,
          raw: `${ex.jp} (${ex.id})`,
        })),
      };
    });

    return {
      id: `${spec.id}-bab-${ch.chapterNumber}`,
      bookId: spec.id,
      topicJp: ch.topicJp,
      topicId: ch.topicId,
      chapterNumber: ch.chapterNumber,
      titleJp: ch.titleJp,
      titleId: ch.titleId,
      patterns,
    };
  });

  const totalPatterns = chapters.reduce((sum, ch) => sum + ch.patterns.length, 0);

  return {
    id: spec.id,
    title: spec.title,
    shortTitle: spec.shortTitle,
    japaneseSubtitle: spec.japaneseSubtitle,
    level: spec.level,
    chapters,
    totalPatterns,
  };
}
